5106. matāpimāṉam cucātimāṉam tēcāpimāṉam.
Love of father, caste, and country.
5107. matiyāta vācalil mitiyātiruppatē uttamam.
It is not well to tread even on the threshold of a house in which you
are not respected.
5108. matiyum umatu vitiyum umatu.
Thy purpose and thy destiny.
The settled judgment, and the decrees of God, are in harmony.
5109. matiyai mīṉ cūḻntatu pōla.
As the stars surround the moon.
5110. matil mēl irukkiṟa pūṉai pōl irukkiṟāṉ.
He is like a cat on a wall.
Spoken of one who makes the most of his position.
5111. matu pintu kalakam pōl irukkiṟatu.
Like the uproar of a honey drop.
5112. maturaikku vaḻi vāyil irukkiṟatu.
The way to Madura is in the mouth.
5113. mantiram kāl mati mukkāl.
The incantation is one fourth, and common sense three fourths.
5114. mantirattil māṅkāy viḻumā?
Will mangoes fall by a charm ?
5115. mantiram illāṉ pūcai anti paṭumaḷavum.
Religious ceremonies not regulated by a form, will continue till
sun-set.
5116. mantirikku aḻaku varum poruḷ uraittal.
It is the attribute of a minister to foretell things likely to occur.
5117. mantiri illā yōcaṉaiyum āyutam illāc cēṉaiyum keṭum.
Decisions without councillors, and troops without arms, will perish
5118. mantaiyilum pāl vīṭṭilum tayirā?
Do you expect milk in tħe fold, and curds at home?
5119. mayir uḷḷa cīmāṭṭi vāri muṭikkiṟāḷ.
The damsel who has a fine head of hair, combs and dresses it.
5120. mayir ūṭāṭātār naṭpuc ciṟitu poruḷ ūṭāṭak keṭum.
Friendship so close that a hair cannot be intruded between the parties,
will be destroyed if money matters interpose.
5121. mayir cuṭṭuk kariyākiṟatā?
Can charcoal be formed by burning hair?
5122. mayir piḷakka vakaitēṭiṉāṟpōla.
Like seeking means to split a hair.
5123. mayilāppūr ēri uṭaittuppōkiṟatu eṉṟāl, varukiṟa kamiṭṭikku ākaṭṭum
eṉṟāṟpōla.
Like saying it may be deferred to the next committee, on hearing that
the Mylapore tank has given way.
5124. mayilē mayilē eṉṟāl iṟaku koṭukkumā?
If you exclaim O peacock, O peacock, will it give you its feather?
5125. mayiṟ kaṇṇikku macakkai, māppiḷḷaikku avastai.
The peacock-eyed bride is phrensied, her bridegroom is in anguish.
5126. maraṇattilum keṭṭa mārkkattukkup payappaṭu.
Be more afraid of a vicious course than of death.
5127. maraṇattiṟku vaḻi maṭṭillai.
There are endless ways that lead to death.
5128. marattil iruntu viḻuntavaṉai māṭu mitittatu pōla.
As an ox trampled under foot a man that bad fallen from a tree.
5129. marattup paḻam marattu aṭiyilē viḻum.
The fruit of a tree will fall at its foot.
5130. marattai ilai kākkum māṉattaip paṇam kākkum.
Leaves cover a tree, money covers one's nakedness.
5131. maranāyilē puḻuku vaḻikkalāmā?
Can you obtain musk from a polecat?
5132. maram taṉṉai veṭṭukiṟavaṉukku niḻal koṭukkum.
The tree affords shade to the man who is felling it.
5133. maram veṭṭukiṟavaṉukku niḻalum, maṇ tōṇṭukiṟavaṉukku iṭamumkoṭukkum.
The tree affords shade to him who fells it, the earth supportshim who
digs it.
5134. maram ēṟik kai viṭṭavaṉum, kaṭaṉ vāṅkik kaṭaṉ koṭuttavaṉum keṭṭāṉ. He who let
go his hold after climbing a tree, and he who borrowed money to lend,
came to grief.
5135. maram vaittavaṉ taṇṇīr vārppāṉ.
He who planted the tree will water it.
5136. mariyātai illātāṉ makimai aṟṟāṉ.
He who was not respectful lost his reputation.
5137. mariyāḷ kuṭittaṉam cariyāyp pōccu.
Mary's domestic life has come to an end.
5138. maruṇṭavaṉ kaṇṇukku iruṇṭatellām pēy.
To a gloomy eye all obscure things are demons.
5139. maruttu māvukkuc cayikkiṉai, maṭaṅkāk kutiraikkuc cavukkaṭi.
A sign to a fleet horse, whipping to an obstinate one.
5140. maruntu kāl mati mukkāl.
Medicine one fourth, Common sense three fourths.
5141. maruntum viruntum mūṉṟu nāḷ.
Three days for testing a medicine, and for a feast.
5142. maruntē āyiṉum viruntōṭu uṇ.
Though the quantity be little, like medicine, distribute before eating
it.
5143. marumakaṉukkeṉṟu camaittatai makaṉukku iṭṭu vayiṟu erintāḷām.
It is said that what she cooked for her son-in-law she gave to her son,
and was distressed about it.
5144. maruvil niṉṟa cāppāṭṭai laṅkaṇattil niṉaittukkoṇṭatu pōla.
Like remembering, when hungry, the food of the marriage feast.
5145. malaṭikkut teriyumā piḷḷaiyaip peṟṟa arumai?
Does the barren woman understand the pains of parturition ?
5146. malaṭi aṟivāḷā piḷḷai arumai?
Does the barren woman know the endearments of children?
5147. malaṭiyaip piḷḷai peṟac coṉṉāl peṟuvāḷā?
If the barren woman be asked to bring forth a child, will she do so?
5148. malaril maṇamum, eḷḷil eṇṇeyum, uṭalil uyirum kalantatu pōla.
All pervading, like fragrance in a flower, oil in sesamum seed, and life
in the body.
5149. malinta paṇṭam kaṭaiyilē varum.
When commodities are abundant, they come to market.
5150.malai attaṉai cuvāmikkut tiṉai attaṉai puṣpam.
A flower, as small as a millet-seed, is dedicated to an idol as large as
a mountain.
5151. malai attaṉai cuvāmikku malai attaṉai puṣpam pōṭukiṟārkaḷā?
Do they dedicate flowers as large as mountains, to idols as large a
mountains?
5152. malai ilakkāṉāl kuruṭaṉum eyvāṉ.
If a mountain be the target, even a blind man may shoot.
5153. malai ucciyil kal ēṟṟutal aritu.
It is difficult to roll a stone to the top of a hill.
5154. malai ēṟiṉālum maittuṉaṉaik kai viṭātē.
Though you ascend the mountains, do not leave behind your
brother-in-law, or the son of your maternal uncle.
5155. malaittēṉ muṭavaṉukku varumā?
Will mountain honey come to the lameman?
5156. malai nellikkāykkum kaṭal uppukkum uṟavu ceytavar ār?
Who created the affinity between the mountain nelli, fruit, and sea-salt
?
5157. malaipōlap pirāmaṇaṉ pōkiṟāṉām, piṉ kuṭumikkuaḻukiṟāḷām.
It is said that when a brahman who was equal to a mountain was dying,
his wife was weeping for his tuft of hair.
5158. malai pōla vantatellām paṉipōla nīṅkum.
All that has come upon thee like mountains, shall pass away as dew.
5159. malai muḻaṅkic cuṇṭeli peṟṟatupōla.
As a mountain amidst thunder brought-forth a mouse.
5160. malaimēl iruppāraip paṉṟi pāyvatu uṇṭā?
Can the wild hog rush on thesewho are on the mountain top?
5161. malaiyil viḷaintālum uralil maciyavēṇṭum.
Although produced on the mountains, the rice must be prepared for use in
a mortar.
5162. malaiyē maṇṇāṅkaṭṭi ākiṟapōtu, maṇṇāṅkaṭṭi eppaṭiākum?
When a mountain becomes a sod, what will the sod be like ?
5163. malaiyē viḻuntālum talaiyē tāṅkavēṇṭum.
Should a mountain fall, the head must bear it.
5164. malaiyaik kalli eli piṭittatu pōla.
Like excavating a mountain and catching a rat.
5165. malaiyaic cuṟṟi aṭittavaṉaic ceṭiyaic cuṟṟi aṭiyēṉā?
After having chased and beaten him round a mountain, will it be
difficult to do so round a bush?
5166. malaiyait tuḷaikkac ciṟṟuḷi pōtātā?
Is not asmall chisel sufficient to perforate a rock?
5167. malaiyait tuḷaikka vācci uḷi vantāṟpōla.
As if an adze and chisel came to perforate the rock.
5168. malaiyaip pārttu nāy kulaittāl malaikkuk kēṭō nāykkuk kēṭō?
If a dog bark at a mountain, will the mountain be injured or the dog?
5169. malaiyai malai tāṅkum maṇṇāṅkaṭṭi tāṅkumā?
A rock supports a rock, can a sod do so ?
5170. malai viḻuṅki ammaiyārukkuk katavu cuṇṭāṅkiyā?
Will a door be a difficulty to my aunt who has swallowed a mountain?
5171. malai viḻuṅkutaṟku maṇṇāṅkaṭṭi paccaṭiyā?
Is a sod used as a chutney, to swallow a mountain ?
5172. mallāntu umiḻntāl mārmēlē.
When one spits lying on his back, the spittle will fall on his breast.
5173. mavuṉam malaiyaic cātikkum.
Silence may defy a mountain.
5174. mav iṭap pav āyiṟṟu.
ma being elided, pa has appeared.
5175. maḻaikkāl iruṭṭāṉālum manti kompu iḻantu pāyumā?
Even in the darkness of the rainy season, will a monkey when leaping,
miss the branch?
5176. maḻaikkut taṇṇīr moṇṭu vārppavar ār?
Who draws and pours water into the clouds?
5177. maḻaikkup paṭal kaṭṭic cārttalāmā?
Can you put up a hurdle to keep out the rain ?
5178. maḻaikkō paṭal, iṭikkō paṭal?
Is the hurdle to keep out rain, or the thunderbolt ?
5179. maḻai peytu niṟaiyātatu moṇṭu vārttāl niṟaiyumā?
Can that be filled with a watering pot which cannot be filled by the
rain?
5180. maḻai mukam kāṇāta payirum, tāy mukam kāṇāta piḷḷaiyum.
Vegetation without rain, a child without a mother.
5181. maḻaiyum piḷḷaippēṟum makā tēvarukkum teriyātu.
Even Mahadeva does not know when it will rain, nor when a child will be
born.
5182. maḻai viṭṭum tūvāṉam viṭa illai.
Though the rain has ceased, the drizzling has not.
5183. maṟanta uṭaimai makkaḷukkum ākātu.
An article forgotten is not good even for one's children.
5184. maṟantu cettēṉ pirāṇaṉ vā eṉṟāl varumā?
If one say, I died through forgetfulness, will life return ?
5185. maṉakkacaṭu aṟa māyai nāṭēl.
Seek not empty pleasures to purify the heart.
5186. maṉak kavalai palak kuṟaivu.
Mental anxiety will diminish one's strength.
5187. maṉaccāṭci kuṟṟu mēl maṟu cāṭci vēṇṭām.
If the conscience condemn, other evidence is unnecessary.
5188. maṉatilē pakai utaṭṭilē uṟavu.
Enmity at heart, friendship on the lips.
5189. maṉatil irukkum rakaciyam matikēṭaṉukku vākkilē.
A secret that should be concealed in the mind is uttered by a fool.
5190. maṉatiṟku maṉatē cāṭci, maṟṟataṟkut teyvam cāṭci.
The heart is its own witness, God is the witness of the rest.
5191. maṉatu aṟiyāp poy uṇṭā ?
Can a mind be ignorant of its own falsehood?
5192. maṉattuyar aṟṟōṉ tiṉaccepam uṟṟōṉ.
He who utters prayers daily has no anxiety of mind.
5193. maṉam koṇṭatu māḷikai.
That which is agreeable to the mind is a palace.
5194. maṉam taṭumāṟēl.
Be not confused.
5195. maṉam iruntum caṟṟu vakai aṟṟup pōvāṉ.
Although willing he will lack means.
5196. maṉitar kāṇum poḻutu mavuṉam, irāta poḻutil urutti rāṣappūṉai.
Silent in the presence of man, in their absence a beaded cat.
5197. maṉitaṉ maṟappāṉ, kuṟaipaṭuvāṉ, māṟuvāṉ, pōvāḷ.
Man forgets, is reduced in circumstances, changes and vanishes.
5198. maṉuṣaṉ talaiyai māṉ talai ākkukiṟāṉ māṉ talaiyai maṉuṣaṉ talai ākkukiṟāṉ.
He can transform a man's head into the head of a deer, and he can make a
man's head out of a deer's head.
5199. maṉaiyāḷ viṭiyumuṉ eḻuntu vīṭṭup paṇ ceyvāḷ.
A wife gets up before day-break and looks after her domestic affairs.
5200. maṉaiyāḷukku uṟṟatu oṉṟum collavēṇṭām, māṟṟaṉai oru nāḷum nampa vēṇṭām.
Do not disclose your secrets to your wife, nor trust an enemy at any
time.
5201. maṉō viyātikku maruntu uṇṭā ?
Is there any remedy for mental sickness?
5202. maṉṉavarkaḷ cettārkaḷ, mantirikaḷ cettārkaḷ muṉ iruntōr ellām muṭintārkaḷ.
Kings have perished, their prime ministers have perished, and all who
lived before, are dead.
5203. maṉṉavarkaḷ āṇṭatu ellām mantirikaḷ āṇmai
The government of kings depends on the vigor of their councillors.
5204. maṉṉavarkku aḻaku ceṅkōṉ muṟaimai.
A sceptre of justice is the beauty of a king.
5205. mā irukkiṟa maṇattaip pōl allavō kūḻ irukkiṟakuṇam.
As is the flour so is the gruel.
5206. mā uṇṭāṉāl paṇikāram cuṭalām.
If there be flour, cakes may be baked.
5207. mā ēṟa malai ēṟum.
The accretion of a particle to a mountain increases its size.
5208. māṭu tiruppiṉavaṉ arccuṉaṉ.
He who drove back the cows was Arjuna.
5209. māṭu tiṉṉikku vākkuc cuttam uṇṭā ?
Is a beef-eater accustomed to decent words?
5210. māṭu mukkivara vīṭu nakkivarum.
When cows return fatigued the household will suffer want.
5211. māṭu mēykkāmaṟ keṭṭatu, payir pārkkāmaṟ keṭṭatu.
Without pasture cows die; without care vegetation perishes.
5212. māṭṭiṉ vāḻkkai mūṭṭaiyilē.
A bull's term of existence is in his sack.
5213. māṭṭukkup peyar periya kaṭā eṉṟu.
A cow is otherwise called a large he-goat.
5214. māṭṭaip pul uḷḷa talattilum, maṉuṣaṉaic cōṟu uḷḷatalattilum irukka oṭṭātu.
It will not allow the cow to be content in its pasture, nor a man with
his boiled rice.
5215. māṭṭai mēyttāṉām kōlaip pōṭṭāṉām.
It is said that he tended the cows and threw away the goad.
5216. māṇikkak kālukku māṟṟukkāl irukkiṟatu.
There is a spare leg, for the one made of a ruby.
5217. mātam kātavaḻi māṉākat tuḷḷuvāṉ.
He will leap like a deer, ten miles in a month.
5218. mātā ceytatu makkaḷukku.
The faults of a mother are visited on her children.
5219. mātā maṉam eriya vāḻāḷ oru nāḷum.
Whilst her mother's heart is wounded, she will not prosper even for a
day.
5220. māttirai tappa mitittāl, kōttiram kūṟappaṭum.
If one exceed his limit, his ancestry will be exposed.
5221. māp puḷikkiṟatu ellām paṇikārattukku nalam.
The more the flour is leavened the better for the cakes.
5222. māp poṉ irukka makkaḷaic cāvak koṭuppēṉā?
Whilst I possess a particle of gold, will I allow my children to die?
5223. mā maṟanta kūḻukku uppu oru kēṭā?
Does the absence of salt spoil gruel made without flour?
5224. māmi oṭṭiṉālum pāṉai oṭṭātu.
Though a mother-in-law may be reconciled, the broken pieces of any
earthen pot cannot be reunited.
5225. māmiyār uṭai kulaintāl vāyālum collakkūṭātu, kaiyālum kāṭṭakkūṭātu.
If the dress of a mother-in-law be out of order, it must not be spoken
of, or pointed at by the hand.
5226. māmiyār uṭaittāl maṇkalam, marumakaḷ uṭaittāl poṉkalam.
If broken by the mother-in-law it is an earthen vessel, if by the
daughter-in-law, it is a golden vessel.
5227. māmiyāraik kaṇṭu marumakaṉ nāṇukiṟatupōla.
As the son-in-law is embarrassed in the presence of his mother in-law.
5228. māmiyār cetta āṟām mātam marumakaḷ kaṇṇil kaṇṇīr vantatām.
It is said that six months after the death of the mother-in-law, a tear
came into the eye of the daughter-in-law.
5229. māmiyār cettu marumakaḷ aḻukiṟatu pōla.
Like the wailing of a daughter-in-law, on account of the death of her
mother-in-law.
5230. māmiyārum cākāḷō maṉakkavalaiyum tīrātō?
Will my mother-in-law never die, will my sorrows never end?
5231. māmiyār vīṭu makā ceḷakkiyam.
The house of the mother-in-law is very comfortable.
5232. māyakkārar ellām pātakar, māṟupāṭṭukkārar ellām cātakar.
All impostors are perfidious villains, all double-dealers are
practically so.
5233. māyakkāraṉ pōyiṟ kaṭaiyē.
A hypocrite is worse than a demon.
5234. māri allatu kāriyam illai.
Without rain nothing can be effected.
5235. māri kālattil patiṉ kala mōrum, kōṭai kālattil orupaṭi nīrum cari.
Ten kalams of buttermilk in the wet season, are worth one measure of
water in the hot season.
5236. mār aṭitta kūli maṭimēlē.
The hire for beating their breasts is at once on the lap.
5237. mārpu carintāl vayiṟu tāṅka vēṇṭum.
If the breasts be pendent, they must be supported by the stomach.
5238 mārpait taṭṭi maṉatilē vai.
Touch your breast, and keep it in your mind.
5239. mālai kuḷittu maṉaiyil pukum, taṉ maṉaiyil āraiyum cērkātu.
In the evening a crow bathes before it goes to its nest, and will not
admit a stranger.
Many Natives attribute to the crow five peculiarities of which that
indicated in this proverb is one. The peculiarities, or habits are as
follows :- (a) Going forth very early of a morning. (b) Never being seen
to pair. (c) Eating together. (d) Bathing before going to their west.
(e) Warning their companions of apparent danger.
5240. mālai cuṟṟip peṇ piṟantāl māmaṉukku ākātu.
For a girl to be born with a garland round her neck is ominous to her
maternal uncle.
This mālai may be either the navel-string or a thin membrane that covers
the head, and which, sometimes decends like a ring, to the neck, it is
then called mālai, if otherwise mukamūṭi a veil.
5241. mālai cuṟṟip piṟanta piḷḷai māmaṉukku ākātu.
If a child be born with its navel string round its neck, it is ominous
to its uncle.
5242. māvilum oṭṭalām māṅkāyilum oṭṭalām.
A share in the dough, a share in the mangos.
5243. māvukku takka paṇikāram.
Cakes proportioned to the flour.
5244. māvait tiṉṟāl appam illai.
If you eat the dough you will not get your share of cake.
5245. māṟṟilē vaḷaivatu marattilē vaḷaiyumā?
Will the tree be pliable because the sapling was so?
5246. māṟṟāṉukku iṭam koṭēl.
Yield not to a foe.
5247. māṉattiṉ mēlē kaṇṇum māppiḷḷai mēlē cintaiyum.
The eye is on the heavens, the mind is on the bridegroom.
5248. māṉattai villāy vaḷaippāṉ maṇalaik kayiṟāyt tirippāṉ.
He is able to bend the sky as a bow, and he can twist ropes of sand.
5249. māṉam aḻiyil uyir kāvalā?
When honour is perishing, is life worth preservation?
5250. māṉam peritō , cīvaṉ peritō?
Is honour great, or is life ?
5251. māṉilam cilarait tāṅkēṉ eṉṉumō?
Does the spacious earth refuse to uphold any one?
5252. māṉukku oru puḷḷi ēṟi eṉṉa, kuṟaintu eṉṉa?
What matters it whether the deer has more or fewer spots ?
5253. māṉuṣiyam illātavaṉ maṉuṣap patar.
Among mankind one destitute of humanity is as chaff.
5254. māṉ kaṇṇilum aḻaku viraivilum viraivu.
More beautiful than the eye of a deer, more rapid than its speed.
5255.māṉ kūṭṭattil puli pukuntatupōla,
As the tiger sprang on a herd of deer.
5256. mikuti ācai atika naṣṭam.
The greater the inordinate deșire, the greater the loss.
5257. mikuti uḷḷavaṉukkum vañcaṉaikkāraṉukkum pakai, vētakkāraṉukkum ulakattiṟkum
pakai.
Enmity exists between the wealthy and the hypocrite, and between a
religious man and the world.
5258. mikuntum kuṟaintum nōy ceyyum.
Both, excess and want, lead to disease.
5259. mikaipaṭac collēl.
Do not exaggerate.
5260. miñciṉatu koṇṭu mēṟkē pōkal ākātu.
It is not good for one to go westward carrying refuse.
5261. miñciya karumam añcac ceyyum.
A thing done through inadvertence may produce fear.
5262. miṭi itayaṅkoḷ mīḷāk kati toṭar.
Be humble, and seek unfailing bliss.
5263. miṭimaiyilum paṭimai naṉṟu.
The habiliments of an ascetic are to be preferred before poverty.
5264. miṭukkaṉ carakku irukka vilaippaṭum.
The haughty sells his goods sitting.
5265. mitittāraik kaṭiyāta pāmpu uṇṭā ?
Are there any snakes that will not bite those who tread on them?
5266. mirukaṅkaḷil āṉai peritu, atilum ciṅkam valitu.
Among beasts an elephant is the biggest, and a lion the strongest.
5267. miṉukku uḷḷa ampu tuṉpam ceyyum. atupōla, antam uḷḷavarkaḷum tuṉpattait
taruvārkaḷ.
A shining arrow will occasion pain, in like manner, those who are
handsome in person may produce pain.
5268. miṉaikkeṭṭa ampaṭṭaṉ pūṉaiyaic ciraittāṉām.
It is said that a barber who had nothing to do, shaved a cat.
5269. miṉmiṉippūcci veḷiccattukku iruḷpōmō?
Does a fire fly dispel darkness ?
5270. miṉṉalaippōl pallai viḷakkāṉum, miṉukkik koḷvāṉum patar.
He who will not clean his teeth so as to shine like lightning, and he
who is fond of show, are chaff.
5271. miṉṉāmal iṭi viḻumā?
Does the thunder-bolt fall without previous lightning ?
5272. miṉṉāmal maḻai peyyumā?
Does it rain without previous lightning?
5273. miṉṉāmal muḻaṅkāmal iṭi viḻuntatu pōla.
As a thunder-bolt fell without lightning and thunder.
5274. miṉṉuvatellām poṉ alla.
All that glitters is not gold.
5275. miṉṉukku ellām piṉṉē maḻai.
Lightning is always followed by rain.
5276. mīkāmaṉ illā marakkalam ōṭātu.
An unpiloted vessel will not sail.
5277. mītūṇ virumpēl.
Be not gluttonous.
5278. mīnta cuṇṇāmpaiyum melinta rājāvaiyum kaiviṭal ākātu.
The remains of chunambu, and an enfeebled king, should not be renounced.
5279. mīṉai mīṉ viḻuṅkiṉāṟpōl.
Like a fish swallowing a fish.
5280. mīṉ kuñcukku nīccup paḻakkukiṟatā?
Are young fish taught to swim?
5281. mīṉ kuḻampō tēṉ kuḻampō?
Fish curry or honey curry?
5282. mukak kōṇalukkuk kaṇṇāṭi pārttāl tīrumā?
Will the distortion of the countenance be removed by looking into a
mirror?
5283. mukattukku mukam kaṇṇāṭi.
One face is a mirror to another face.
5284. mukattukku añci mūttārōṭu pōṉāl kulattukku ellām īṉamām.
If a woman elope with her husband's elder brother out of personal regard
for him, itwill be a disgrace to the whole family.
5285. mukam ākātiruntāl kaṇṇāṭi eṉṉa ceyyum?
If the face be ugly, what can the mirror do ?
5286. mukam cantira pimpam , akam pāmpiṉ viṣam.
A face like the moon, a mind of deadly poison.
5287. mukkāṭṭukkuḷ camutāṭā?
What! a dagger under a veil?
5288. mukkāṭṭukkuḷḷē kaikkāṭṭā?
What, is it to make signs under a veil?
5289. mukkātam cumantālum mucal kaittūkkuttāṉ.
Though carried thirty miles, a hare is carried in the hand.
5290. mukkālam kākam muḻukik kuḷittālum kokku ākātu.
Though a crow bathe three times a day, it will not thereby become a
white crane.
5291. mukkālam kākam muḻukik kuḷittālum kokku ākumā?
Will a crow by bathing three times a day become a crane?
5292. mukkūṭṭuc cikku aṟātu.
A triparty business is always involved.
5293. mucalai eḻuppi viṭṭu, nāy patuṅkiṉatupōla.
Like a dog crouching after starting a hare.
5294. muṭappul mukkala nīrait taṭukkum.
Crooked grass prevents the flow of six kalams of water.
5295. muṭavaṉukku noṇṭi caṇṭappiracaṇṭaṉ.
Alame man is very boisterous before a cripple.
5296.muṭavaṉ komput tēṉukku ācaippaṭṭatupōla.
As a lame man longed for the honey that hung from a branch.
5297. muṭavaṉ cantaikkup pōṉāṟpōla.
Like a lame man going to market.
5298. muṭikkātavaṉē paṭikkātavaṉ.
He who does not accomplish his object lacks training.
5299. muṭiccup pōṉatum allāmal iḷittavāyppaṭṭamum kūṭak kiṭaittatu.
You have not only lost the bundle of money, but also incurred a
reproachful name.
5300. muṭiya muṭiyanaṭṭāl kōṭṭai kōṭṭaiyāy viḷaiyumā?
If you plant bundles, will heaps be produced?
5301. muṭiyum vakai yōciyāmal muyaṟci koḷḷātē.
Never undertake a matter without due consideration as to how it is to be
accomplished.
5302. muṭi vaitta talaikkuc cuḻicuttam pārkkiṟatu, koṇṭu kulam pēcukiṟatu pōl
irukkiṟatu.
Examining the circlets of hair in a crowned head, is like discussing the
tribe of a girl after marrying her.
5303. muṭṭa naṉaintavaṉukku īram illai, muḻutum keṭṭavaṉukkut tukkam illai.
When completely drenched one does not feel wet, when entirely ruined one
feels no sorrow.
5304. muṭṭarōṭāṭiya naṭpuk karuṅkālik kaṭṭai ūṭāṭiya kāl.
Friendship with the rude is like the foot of a traveller among stumps of
ebony.
5305. muṭṭaṟṟa peṇṇukku iraṭṭaip pariyamā?
What double dower to a worthless woman?
5306. muṭṭāḷukku iraṇṭu āḷ.
Two persons for one fool.
5307. muṭṭāḷukkuk kōpam mūkkiṉ mēlē.
The wrath of a fool is on his nose.
5308. muṭṭikaipōla muṉiyātu vaikalum, koṭṭi uṇpārum kuṟaṭupōṟ kaiviṭuvār.
Even those who like a pair of pincers uphold their dependants and daily
feed them, will leave them like a pair of tongs.
5309. muṭṭi ūṭṭiṉa kaṉṟu mutark kaṉṟu.
A calf that drains the udder is almost weaned.
5310. muṭṭukku muṭṭalla mūṭak katavum alla, caṉṉitivācalukkuc cārttak katavumalla.
It is neither an obstruction, nor a door to shut, nor even mani screen
to the temple gateway.
5311. muṭṭuppaṭṭum jeyam varumāṉāl kuṭṭuppaṭṭāṟ kuṟai eṉṉa ?
If victory comes by being pressed with want, what matters a cuff on the
head?
5312. mutalil eṭuttuc celaviṭātē.
Do not spend on your capital.
5313. mutalilē keṭṭikkāraṉ muṭivilē cōmpēṟi.
Clever at the beginning, indolent at the end.
5314. mutalukku mōcamāka irukkiṟatu ilāpattukkuc caṇṭai pōṭukiṟatā?
When the principal is in danger, do you quarrel about the interest?
5315. mutalē turppalai atilē karppiṇi.
Already weak, and withal pregnant.
5316. mutalaikku illai nīccum nilaiyum.
A crocodile cares not whether the water is deep or shallow.
5317. mutalai taṉ iṭattu, malai otta āṉaiyaiyum iḻuttuc cellum.
In his own element, the alligator will carry off an elephant as big as a
mountain.
5318. mutalai vaittup perukkāta vaṇikaraippōla.
Like merchants who do not increase the capital they invest.
5319. mutal illārkku ūtiyam illai, matalaiyām cārpillārkku nilai illai.
Those who have no capital have no gain, those who have no sons to lean
on, have no support.
5320. mutal illārkku ūtiyam illai.
Those not possessed of capital, have no gains.
5321. mutal eṭukkum pōtē tappaṭṭaikkāraṉ cettāṉ.
When the funeral procession was moving forward, the tomtom beater died.
5322. mutal eḻuttilē veḷḷeḻuttā?
What, dim-sighted at the beginning of the Alphabet?
5323. mutal kōṇal muṟṟum kōṇal.
If crooked at first, it will be so throughout.
5324. mutukilē puṇ uṇṭāṉāl ceṭiyilē nuḻaiyap payam.
If one has a wound on his back, he will fear to pass under a bush.
5325. muttāl nattai perumaippaṭum, mūṭar ettālum perumaippaṭār.
As snail is precious by reason of its pearl, fools have naught where
with to attain greatness.
5326.muttilum cottai uṇṭu , pavaḻattilum paḻutu uṇṭu.
Flaws may be found in pearls and also in coral.
5327. munti vanta ceviyaip pinti vanta kompu maṟaittatām.
It is said that the ears which came first, were covered by the
hornswhich sprung up afterwards.
5328. muntiṉōr pintiṉōr āvar pintiṉōr muntiṉōr āvar.
The first shall be last, and the last first.
5329. muntiṉa cōttait taṭṭiṉāl pintiṉa cōṟu pīyum cōṟum.
If the first food be rejected, worse may be offered.
5330. munnāḻi kaṟakkiṟa pacuvāṉālum iṟappaip piṭuṅkukiṟa pacu ākātu.
Though a cow yields three measures of milk, it is not desirable it pulls
down the roof.
5331. muppatilē mūrkkam, nāṟpatilē nākarīkam.
Obstinacy at thirty, civility at forty.
5332. muppatu paṇam koṭuttālum mūḷippaṭṭam pōkātu.
Thoughone may give thirty fanams, the nickname, crop-eared, willnot be
removed.
5333. muppatilē vāḻntavaṉ mūṭaṉ, muṉṉum piṉṉum teriyātavaṉ kuruṭaṉ.
He is a fool who prospered at thirty, he is blind who does not see
before and behind.
5334. muppaṇi iṭṭa peṇṇukkuk koppu oṉṟutāṉ kuṟai.
To the woman adorned with three jewels, the ear ornament is the only
want.
5335. muppatu varuṣam vāḻntavaṉum illai, muppatu varuṣam tāḻntavaṉum illai.
None ever continued to prosper or decay for thirty years.
5336. mupporuḷ āti mūlamāṉavaṉ.
He is the triad who is the first and the last of all.
5337. muyaṟci uṭaiyār ikaḻcci aṭaiyār.
The industrious will never be put to shame.
5338. muraṭṭup peṇṇum curuṭṭup pāyum.
A stubborn wife, a mat rolled up.
5339. murukkup paruttu eṉṉa tūṇākap pōkiṟatā?
What if the murukku tree grow large, will it do for a pillar?
5340. muruṅkaikkāy eṉṟāl pattiyam muṟiyumā?
Will the mention of the murungai fruit affect a prescribed diet?
5341. mulai koṭuttu vaḷarttavaḷ mūtēvi, muṉṟāṉai pōṭṭavaḷcītēvi.
The one that nursed and brought up the child is Mudevi, the wife is
Shridevi.
5342. mulaik kuttuc cavalaip piḷḷaikkut teriyumā?
Is the pain in the breast of the nurse, known to the suckling ?
5343. muḻaṅkaiyiṟ paṭṭa cukampōla.
Like the pleasure experienced when the elbow is struck.
5344. muḻuki muppatu nāḷāccu , iṟaṅki uppu aḷḷakkūṭa illai eṉkiṟāṉ.
It is thirty days since he bathed, and he says that he is so clean that
touching salt would defile him.
5345. muḻuk kaṭṭi peyarkkiṟa paṉṟikkuk koḻuk kaṭṭi viṭṭatu pōla.
Like arming a hog in the snout with a ploughshare, that can tare up the
ground without it.
5346. muḻuc cōmpēṟi muḷḷu uḷḷa vēli.
A perfect sluggard is like a hedge of thorns.
5347. muḻup paṅkukkāraṉukku muntirip paṅkukkāraṉ miṇṭaṉ.
He whose share is only one three hundred and twentieth part, is more
persistent than he who has a whole onė.
5348. muḻup pūcaṇikkāyaic cōṟṟilē maṟaikkiṟāppōla.
Like attempting to conceal a whole pumpkin in a plate of rice.
5349. muḻu maṇip pūṇukkup pūṇ vēṇṭumō?
Does a gemmed ferrule require an ornamental rim ?
5350. muḷaitta mayir mūṉṟu atilum iraṇṭu puḻuveṭṭu.
His beard consists of three hairs, of which two are rotten at the root.
5351. muḷaiyil kiḷḷātatai muṟṟiṉāl kōṭari koṇṭu veṭṭa vēṇṭum.
That which was not nipped in the bud will have to be felled with an axe
when matured.
5352. muḷḷālē muḷḷai eṭukkavēṇṭum.
Thorns are extracted by thorns.
5353. muḷḷukkuk kūrmaiyum tuḷacikku vācaṉaiyum iyaṟkai.
By nature the thorn is sharp, and the tulasi fragrant.
5354. muḷḷukku muṉai cīvi viṭuvārkaḷā?
Who sharpens the point of a thorn?
5355. muḷḷu mēl cīlai pōṭṭāl meḷḷa meḷḷa vāṅkavēṇṭum.
If a cloth be spread on a thorn bush, it must be taken off with great
care.
5356. muṟattaṭi paṭṭālum mukattaṭi paṭalākātu.
Though one may endure being struck with a sieve, he cannot endure being
brow-beaten.
5357. muṟpakal ceyyiṟ piṟpakal viḷaiyum.
What is done in the forenoon will result in good or evil in the
afternoon.
5358. muṟṟum naṉaintavarkaḷukku īram ētu?
Do those who are drenched complain of being wet?
5359. muṉaimukattu nillēl.
Do not stand in the face of a battle.
5360. muṉ aḷanta nāḻi piṉ aḷakkum.
The same measure that was used before, must be used afterwards.
5361. muṉ oṉṟu ōtip piṉ oṉṟu āṭēl.
Do not say one thing and do another.
5362. muṉ kai nīṇṭāl muḻaṅkai nīḷum.
If the fore-arm be stretched, the elbow will be so also.
5363. muṉ kōpam piṉ irakkam.
Anger first, and pity afterwards.
5364. muṉ viṭṭup piṉ niṉṟu kaḻuttu aṟukkalāmā?
Having given one the lead, will you follow and cut his throat?
5365. muṉṉavaṉē muṉ niṉṟāl muṭiyāta poruḷ uḷatō?
If the eternal be with you, will anything be impossible ?
5366. muṉṉē pōṉāl kaṭikkiṟatu piṉṉē pōṉāl utaikkiṟatu.
Biting before, and kicking behind.
5367. muṉṉē pār piṉṉē pār uṉṉaip pār eṉṉaip pār.
Look before, look behind, look at yourself, look at me.
5368. muṉṉē pōṉāl cicuvatti piṉṉē pōṉāl piramatti.
When you go before you are guilty of infanticide, when you follow you
are guilty of brahmanicide.
5369. muṉṉēram kappaṟkāraṉ piṉṉēram piccaikkāraṉ.
In the forenoon a ship owner, in the afternoon a beggar.
5370. muṉṉē vanta kātaippārkkilum piṉṉē vanta kompu palam.
The horn that came after, is stronger than the ear that came before.
5371. mūkkilikkuk kaṇṇāṭi kāṭṭiṉatupōla.
Like showing a noseless man a mirror.
5372. mūkkaṟaiyaṉukku vāḻkkaippaṭṭāl muṉṉum pōkaviṭār piṉṉum pōkaviṭāṉ.
If a woman is married to one whose nose is rent, he will not allow her
to go before or after him.
5373. mūkku aṟupaṭṭa kaḻutai tūvāṉattiṟku añcātu.
A crop-nosed ass does not fear driving rain.
5374. mūkkup puṇṇāḷi allavō tācari ākavēṇṭum?
A man with a sore nose ought to become a Vaishnava mendicant, ought he
not?
5375. mūkku mayir piṭuṅkiṉatupōla varuttam varum.
The pain will be felt as keenly as when the hair in the nose is plucked
out.
5376. mūkku mayir piṭuṅkiṉāl āṭ pāram kuṟaiyumā?
Will a person's weight be diminished by pulling the hair out of his nose
?
5377. mūkkaip piṭittāl cīvaṉ pōkiṟatu.
If the nostrils be closed, life will depart.
5378. mūkkaip piṭittāl vāy ā veṉṉat teriyātu?
He knows not how to open his mouth when one closes his nostrils?
5379. mūṅkiṟ pāyum muraṭṭup peṇṭāṭṭiyum.
A bamboo mat, and an obstinate wife.
5380. mūṅkil ilai mēlē tūṅku paṉi nīrē.
Thou art a dew drop depending from the leaf of a bamboo.
5381. mūṭarōṭu āṭiya naṭpuk kaṭuvaḻiyiṟ kaṭṭai ūṭāṭiya kāl.
The friendship of fools is as the feet that have travelled by a jungle
path covered with stumps of trees.
5382. mūṭar muṉpu mūrkkam pēcātē.
Speak not harshly before fools.
5383. mūṭarkaḷ cērkkaiyāl tappāmal keṭuti varum.
The companionship of fools invariably leads to loss.
5384. mūṭar kūṭṭu uṟavu muḻutum apāyam.
Danger attends the friendship of fools.
5385. mūṭaṉāy irukkiṟa piḷḷaiyiṉālē eppōtum naṣṭamē varum.
One always sustains loss if he has a stupid child.
5386. mūṭaṉuṭaiya paṅku tarittiramum ikaḻcciyum.
Adversity and disgrace form the lot of fools.
5387.mūṭaṉ caṇṭai mūṭṭup pirikkum, mōrkkaṭaṉ vīṭṭait toṭum.
The quarreling of fools will break friendship, debt on account of
buttermilk will affect one's house.
5388. mūṭiṉa muttu mūṉṟu lōkam peṟum, mūṭāta muttu mukkāṟ kācum peṟātu.
A pearl concealed is worth the three worlds, one that is uncovered wont
fetch three quarters of a cash.
5389. mūṭṭaikkāraṉukku muḻaṅkālilē putti.
A porter's sense is is his knees.
5390. mūttatu mōḻai iḷaiyatu kāḷai.
The first-born is a hornless animal, the younger is a bull.
5391. mūttōr col vārttai amirtam.
The utterances of elderly persons are ambrosia.
5392. mūppilum tarumam ceytal muyaṟcitāṉ.
The charitably disposed exert themselves even in old age.
5393. mūppu ēṉ piṭippatu, mūtēvi vācattukku aṭaiyāḷam.
Why does, one grow old ? it is a sign that he is under the influence of
the goddess of misfortune.
5394. mūrkka ṉum mutalaiyum cari.
The stubborn and crocodiles are alike.
5395. mūrkkaṉ mukattil mūtēvi kuṭi iruppāḷ.
The goddess of misfortune dwells in the face of the stubborn.
5396. mūrkkaṉaic cērntavaṉ vāḻāṉ, mūṭaṉaic cērntavaṉ paṭiyāṉ.
He who associates with the angry will not prosper, and he who associates
with fools will not learn.
5397. mūrkkam uḷḷa rājāvum mūṭa mantiriyum aḻivārkaḷ.
A king that is easily provoked, and a prime minister wanting discretion,
will come to ruin.
5398. mūrkkarōṭu iṇaṅkēl.
Associate not with the angry.
5399. mūlikai aṟintāl mūṉṟu ulakamum āḷalām.
He who is acquainted with botany may govern the three worlds.
5400. mūṉṟām kaṭṭu aviḻttāl teriyum.
It will be clear if you loose the third knot.
5401. mūṉṟu muḻamum oru cuṟṟu, muppatu muḻamum oru cuṟṟu.
A cloth of thirty cubits is put on with as much ease as one of three
cubits.
5402. mūṉṟu vīṭṭukku mukkāli nālu vīṭṭukku nāṟkāli.
A three legged seat to three houses, and four legged seat, to four
houses.
5403. mūṉṟu peyar vaḻikkut tuṇai, iraṇṭu peyar piṇaiyal māṭu, oruvaṉ pōṉāl
paratēci.
Three may help one another on the way, two are like a yoke of oxen, one
is like a pilgrim.
5404. mūṉṟu poruḷaiyum tēṭu mutirvayatil ūṉṟukōl ākum.
Secure the three things virtue, wealth and happiness, they will serve as
a staff in old age.
5405. mūṉṟē mukkāl nāḻikaikkuḷ muttu maḻai peytatu, vāri eṭukkumuṉṉē maṇmāri
peytatu.
It rained pearls for three and three fourths of a naligai, but before
they could be gathered it rained earth.
5406. mettap paṭittavarukkuc cōṟu vellam.
To the profoundly learned rice is sugar.
5407. mettap paṭittavaṉ paiyittiyakkāraṉ.
He who is very learned, is a fool.
5408. mettap parivām uḷḷē erivām.
Externally sympathising, internally envying.
5409. metteṉap paṭuttal nittiraikku aḻaku.
A soft bed is favourable to sleep.
5410. meykoṇṭu viḻikkiṟatu poykoṇṭu porikiṟatu.
Shining with truth, burning with lies.
5411. mey colli vāḻātāṉ poy colli vāḻvāṉā?
Will he who cannot prosper by truth, prosper by falsehood?
5412. meyt toḻil eṉṟum mey payakkum.
Honest occupation always secures substantial results.
5413. meypporuḷ kalviyē kaipporuḷ.
Learning is real wealth.
5414. mey mūṉṟām piṟai, poy pūraṇa cantiraṉ.
Truth is the crescentof the third day, falsehood is the full moon.
5415. meymai cāṟṟa vaiyam ēṟṟum.
When you speak truth, the world will honour you.
5416. meyyatu naṉṟi iṭum.
Truth is beneficial.
5417. meyyāṉa cattiyaṉ vēta vācakaṉ.
He is the truthful man who knows the vedas.
5418. meyyuṭai oruvaṉ collamāṭṭāmaiyāl poypōlummēpoypōlummē.
Truth in one who cannot speak easily, may appear like falsehood.
5419. melintavaḷukku mettap pelaṉ, mēṉi miṉukku iṭṭavaḷukku mettak kacam.
A lean woman is strong, a gaudy woman is consumptive.
5420. mellap pāyum taṇṇīr kallaiyum kuḻiyap pāyum.
Gently flowing water will hollow even a rock.
5421. melliyāṭōḷ cēr.
Live with your wife.
5422. meḻukiṉa vīṭṭilē nāy pukuntatu pōla.
As a dog entered a house whose floor was smeared with cow-dung.
5423. mēṭṭimaikkārarukku etirttu niṟkavēṇṭum, mettaṉak kāraruk kirupai
aḷikkavēṇṭum.
Submit not to the haughty, and to the humble show pity.
5424. mēṭṭukkākap payamām, vīti vaḻiyil tikilām.
It is said that he is afraid, of the hill, and alarmed at the high road.
5425. mēyap pōkiṟa māṭu kompilē pullaik kaṭṭikkoṇṭu pōkiṟatā?
Do cattle going to graze, carry grass tied to their horns?
5426. mēykiṟa māṭṭai nakkukiṟa māṭu keṭuttāṟpōla.
As the cow that grazes is interrupted by the one that licks it.
5427. mēykiṟa kaḻutaiyaik kūvukiṟa kaḻutai keṭuttatām.
It is said that the braying ass interrupted the ass that was grazing.
5428. mēykkum mēyppaṉai viyakkum vāyaṉ.
He who praises the cowherd.
5429. mēyttāl maittuṉiyai mēyppēṉ illāviṭṭāṟ paratēcam pōvēṉ.
If I am to rule I must rule over my sister-in-law, otherwise I shall go
on a pilgrimage.
5430. mēyttāṟ kaḻutai mēyppēṉ illāviṭṭāl paratēcam pōvēṉ.
I will tend the donkeys, or go on a pilgrimage.
5431. mēlām miṉukkiyaik koṇṭavaṉ keṭṭāṉ, mēṭṭilē payiriṭṭavaṉ keṭṭāṉ.
He who marries a woman of great beauty will be ruined, he who sows on
hilly ground, will be impoverished.
5432. mēlaikku vāḻkkaippaṭukiṟēṉ kaḻuttē cummā iru.
I will marry some time hence; be still till then, my neck.
5433. mēlaikku uḻuvār kūḻukku aḻuvār.
Those who plough late will cry for want of food.
5434. mēḻic celvam kōḻaipaṭātu.
The wealth of the plough is unfailing.
5435. mēṉi oṟuppē ñāṉi niṉaippu.
Sages are intent upon self-denial.
5436. mēṉmakkaḷ coṟ kēḷ.
Listen to the words of the great.
5437. mēṉmaiyiṉ mēṉmaiyaṉ mēlām pataviyaṉ.
The most excellent is the possessor of the highest state of bliss.
5438. mai karaiyāmal mutuku āṭṭu.
Rub your back without spoiling the paint.
5439. maiviḻiyāṭaṉaik kaiyakaṉṟoḻuku.
Live far removed from prostitutes who paint their eye-lids.
5440. maiviḻiyār maṉaiyakal.
Avoid the house of a prostitute.
5441. moṭṭait talaikkum muḻaṅkālukkum muṭi iṭṭatupōla.
Like joining a bald head and the knee, by tying a knot of hair.
An impossibility.
5442. moṭṭait talaiyil paṭṭam kaṭṭi āḷa vantāṉō?
Has he come to reign with a crown on his bald head?
5443. moṭṭait talaiyil pēṉ cērumā?
Will lice attach themselves to a bald head?
5444. moṭṭait talaiyiṟ pēy varumā?
Will a demon come on a bald head?
5445. moṭṭait talaiyiṟ pēṉ pōla.
Like a louse on a bald head.
5446. moṭṭait talaiyaṉ pōrukku añcāṉ.
A bald-headed man fears not to fight.
5447. moṭṭait talaikku oru koṭṭuk kūṭai, mōḻait talaikku oru tāṟṟuk kūṭai.
To a bald head, a cup-shaped basket, to a hornless head, a basket of
goads.
5448. moṭṭait talaiyaṉ muḻu mōcakkāraṉ.
A bald-headed man is a perfect cheat.
5449. moṭṭaiccikkut takunta mūkkaṟaiyaṉ.
A noseless man, fit for a bald woman.
5450. moṇṭu āḷukiṟa vīṭṭil koṇṭu āṇṭāl niṟaiyumā?
Will an affluent household be content to live from hand to mouth?
5451. mottaic cōṟṟukku mēḷam aṭikkiṟāṉ.
He beats a tomtom to get a mouthful of rice.
5452. moḻi tappiṉavaṉ vaḻi tappiṉavaṉ.
A promise breaker is in the wrong way.
5453. moḻi tavaṟātāṉ vaḻi tavaṟātāṉ.
He who is true to his word, swerves not from rectitude.
5454. moḻivatu maṟukkiṉ, aḻivatu karumam.
If one break his promise, his undertaking will fail.
5455. moḻivatu aṟa moḻi.
Speak decisively.
5456. mōkattai muṉi.
Renounce lust.
5457. mōkam muppatu nāḷ ācai aṟupatu nāḷ.
Lust continues thirty days, desire sixty days.
5458. mōkaṉak kal āṉālum paḷu ēṟiṉāl uṭaiyātā?
Will not even a door-step break under excessive pressure?
5459. mōcam pāy pōṭṭut tūṅkukiṟatu.
Danger slumbers on a mat.
5460. mōntukoḷvatu pōl kaṭikkiṟatu.
To bite when apparently kissing.
5461. mōrukkup pōkiṟavarukku muṭṭi piṟakālēyō?
When going for buttermilk why carry the pot concealed behind you?
5462. mōrukkup pōy montaiyai oḷippāṉ ēṉ ?
Why conceal the vessel when going for buttermilk?
5463. mōrō eṉkiṟavaṉ kaḻuttil liṅkam kaṭṭiṉatu pōla.
As a linga was tied to the neck of a buttermilk seller.
5464. mōṉam eṉpatu ñāṉavarampu.
Silence is the bulwark of wisdom.
5465. meḷaṉam kalaka nācam.
Silence puts an end to quarrels.
5466. meḷaṉi kuṭiyaik keṭuppāḷ.
A reserved woman will destroy her family.
5467. yāti muṟṟiṉāl viyāti.
Matured meditation ends in disease.
5468. yāṉai kaṟuttāl āyiram poṉ peṟum, pūṉai kaṟuttāl eṉṉa peṟum?
A black elephant is worth a thousand gold peices, what will a black cat
fetch?
5469. yāṉaikku aṟupatu aṭi , aruṅ kuḷḷaṉukku eḻupatu aṭi.
Sixty feet from an elephant, Seventy from a dwarf.
5470. yāṉai taṉ talaiyilē maṇṇai vārik koṭṭikkoḷkiṟatupōla.
As an elephant throws sand on its head.
5471. yāṉai tiṉṟa viḷāṅkaṉipōla.
Like a blighted wood-apple.
5472. yāṉai mitikkap piḻaippārkaḷā?
Will they survive who have been trampled on by an elephant?
5473. yāṉaimuṉṉē muyal mukkiṉatu pōla.
As a hare strained itself before at elephant.
5474. yāṉaimutalāṉa periya jentukkaḷ tāḻa viḻuntāl piḻaikkā; atupōla, periyōr
kīḻē viḻuntāl tēṟamāṭṭār.
Large beasts such as elephants &c., when they fall down from a high
place, live not, so are the great.
5475. yāṉaiyai āyiram poṉṉukku vāṅki iruppu aṅkucattiṟku ēmāntu niṟpāṉ ēṉ?
Having bought an elephant for a hundred pieces of gold, why hesitate to
buy its iron goad?
5476. yāṉaiyait tēṭak kuṭattukkuṭ kai vaittatu pōla.
As one put his hand into a jar when he was seeking an elephant.
5477. yukamuṭiya maḻai peytālum ōṭṭāṅkiḷiñcil karaiyumā?
Suppose it rain to the end of the Yuga, will a potsherd be thereby
dissolved?
5478. raṇṭāṭṭil ūṭṭiṉ kuṭṭiyāyt tīrntatu.
It turned out to be a kid that had sucked two dams.
5479. laṅkaṇam parama oḷaṣatam.
Abstinence is the best medicine.
5480. lōpikku iru celavu.
Double expense to the niggard.
5481. vaṅkaṇakkāraṉ puḷuku vācaṟpaṭi maṭṭum.
The lies of a paramour reach as far as the door.
5482. vaṅkam tiṉṟāl taṅkam, vaṅkam keṭṭāl paṅkam.
When lead is eaten it becomes gold; when it is spoiled it becomes
useless.
5483. vaṅkiṣam vārttaikku añcum, puḻukkai utaikku añcum.
The high-born are afraid of reproach, a menial is afraid of kicks.
5484. vacaṉam paṇṇa upāyam kāraṇam.
In framing rules tact is required.
5485. vacca nāpiyilē puḻutta puḻu.
A worm produced in poison.
5486. vacca nāpiyai uppup pārkkalāmā ?
Is arsenic to be tasted to ascertain its flavour?
5487. vaccattukku mēlē vaḻi illai, piccaikkup pōkac curaik kuṭukkai illai.
There is no way beyond Vachham, there is no gourd-shell to beg alms.
5488. vañcakarkku eṉṉa nēcam kāṭṭiṉālum neñcu nēcam koḷḷār.
No matter what love is shown to the deceitful, it will not affect the
mind.
5489. vañcar uṟavai vaḻuvi vilaku.
Relinquish intercourse with the deceitful.
5490. vañcar pāl ūṭṭiṉālum nañcāyviṭum.
Even milk given by the deceitful becomes poison.
5491. vañcittu neṭum kālam vāḻtalil maraṇam aṭaivatē nalam.
It is better to die than to live long in a deceitful course.
5492. vaṭakāṟṟu aṭikka vantatu maḻaiyē.
As the wind veered to the north, it began to rain.
Thịs is generally true as regards Madras.
5493. vaṭakkattiyāṉaiyum vayiṟṟu valiyaiyum nampal ākātu.
You must not be heedless of a northman, or of the belly-ache.
5494. vaṭakkē kaṟuttāl maḻai varum.
Darkening in the north betokens rain.
5495. vaṭakkup pārtta maccuvīṭṭaip pārkkilum teṟkup pārtta terut tiṇṇai nallatu.
A pyal facing south is preferable to a terraced house with a northern
aspect.
The following rules relating to the building of a house will illustrate
this proverb.
Having selected a site, the frontage must be divided into nine equal
parts, five being assigned to the right, and three to the left, the
fourth division being reserved for the door-way. The enumeration begins
on the left, and thus the fourth section is in the mansion of Mercury.
The occupant of such a house may become as wealthy as Kuberar.
A person born under Gemini, Cancer or Leo must build his house on a line
stretching east and west, the entrance being placed easterly.
A person bornunder Virgo, Libra or Scorpio, must build ona line running
north and south, the door - waybeing southerly.
One born under Sagittarius, Capricorn or Aquarius must build west and
east placing the entrance westerly. If born under Pices(?), Aries or the
Twins, he must build south and north the door being placed northerly. A
family occupying house built contrary to these rules will be ruined.
5496. vaṭaliyai veṭṭi āḷ, erumaiyaik kaṭṭi āḷ.
Trim the young palmyrah, and tie up the buffalua.
5497. vaṭali vaḷarttuk kaḷḷaik kuṭikkiṟatā ?
Is it in expectation of today that youplant a palmyrah tree?
5498. vaṭukaṉum tamiḻaṉum kūṭṭup payir iṭṭa katai
The story of a Telugu man and a Tamil man, cultivating jointly.
5499. vaṭukaṉ tamiḻ aṟiyāṉ vaikkōlaik kacu eṉpāṉ.
A Telugu man does not understand Tamil, he will call vaikol kasu.
5500. vaṭuku koḻuttāl vaṟai ōṭṭiṟkum ākātu.
If a Telugu man prosper, he is of no use to anyone.
5501. vaṭuku poṭukāccu vaikkōṟ pōr nel āccu.
The slave has become small, the corn-stack is thrashed into paddy.
5502. vaṭaiyait tiṉṉac coṉṉāḷā tuḷaiyai eṇṇac coṉṉāḷā?
Did she tell you to eat the cakes, or to count the holes in them?
5503. vaṭṭam cuṟṟiyum vaḻikku varavēṇṭum.
Although you go round, you must come in by the entrance.
5504. vaṭṭi ācai mutalaik keṭuttatu.
Excessive desire after interest, destroyed the capital.
5505. vaṭṭikku vaṭṭi etir vaṭṭiyā?
Is compound interest uncommon?
5506. vaṭṭi ōṭṭam viḻa ōṭṭattilum atikam.
The speedat which interest accumulates is greater than that of a car.
5507. vaṭṭuvattiṉ mēl coṭṭup pōṭṭāl vaṭṭuvattiṟku māttiramā paṭum?
Will a slap on a betel-pouch, affect the pouch only?
5508. vaṇaṅkiṉa vil tīṅkai viḷaikkum.
A bent bow wil do mischief.
5509. vaṇaṅkiṉa muḷ taikkātu.
A pliant thorn will not penetrate.
5510. vaṇikarkku aḻaku vāṇipam ceytal.
To trade is the beauty of merchants.
5511. vaṇṭi ōṭattiṉ peyaril ōṭam vaṇṭiyiṉ peyaril.
A cart may be seen on a boat, and a boat on a cart.
5512. vaṇṭu tūrattilē pūviṉ vācaṉaiyai aṟiyum; atupōla, kaṟṟōr tūrattilēyē oruvaṉ
naṭattaiyai aṟintu koḷvārkaḷ.
As beetles smell at a distance the fragrance of flowers, so the learned
understand one's character when he is at a distance.
5513. vaṇṭu ēṟāta maram illai.
There is no tree that cannot be bored by a beetle.
5514. vaṇṇattukkuk kiṇṇam pāṭukiṟāṉ.
He sings an unmelodious tune.
5515. vaṇṇāṉukku nōy vantāl kallōṭē.
If a washerman is sick, he gets better at the washing stone.
5516. vaṇṇāṉukkum niruvāṇikkum uṟavu eṉṉa?
What friendship has a washerman with one who wears no clothes?
5517. vaṇṇāṉukku vaṇṇāttimēl ācai, vaṇṇāttikkuk kaḻutai mēl ācai.
The washerman longs for the washer-woman, and the washer woman's desire
is fixed on her donkey.
5518. vaṇṇāṉ kaiyil māṟṟuc cummā .
A change of garments in the hands of the washerman.
5519. vaṇṇāṉ kaiyil cēvaiyaip pōṭṭuk kokkiṉ piṉṉē pōkiṟatā ?
Having put your clothes to the washerman, do you chase the crane?
5520. vaṇṇāṉ piḷḷai cettāl ampaṭṭaṉukku mayir pōyiṟṟu.
If the washermen's child die, the barber cares not a hair.
5521. vaṇṇāṉ tuṟaiyil muyalpōla.
Like a hare at the washerman’s washing place.
5522. vaṇṇāṉukkup pōṉāṉ vaṇṇāttikku vantāṉ.
To the washerman, his name was "gone;' to the washer - woman, "come.”.
This proverb is explained as follows:a washerman engaged a servant who
gave his name as Ponan, he is gone. In the absence of her husband he
told the washer-woman that his name is Vandan, he is come.
The washerman wanting the man called him by name, -Ponan. At the same
moment the wife called the man by his other name Vandan.The washerman
thereupon concluded that the servant had gone to his wife. Again be
called as before, and his wife also called. As the servant did not come
to him, the washerman became angry and went to his wife when an
altercation took place. The matter being explained, they suspected that
something was wrong, and soon found that the manhad made off with their
savings, and that he had given the two names to serve his secret
purpose.
5523.vatuvai ceytu vāḻ.
Get married and live prosperously.
5524. vastirāparaṇam vicēṣamō, aṉṉam vicēṣamō?
Which is more important, dress or food?
5525. vanta kālōṭu pantaṟ kālaik kaṭṭikkoṇṭu irukkiṟāy.
Thou standest embracing the pillar of the pandal, having walked a long
way.
5526. vanta caṇṭaiyai viṭuvatum illai valiyac caṇṭaikkup pōvatum illai.
The quarrel that has ensued is not relinquished, nor is another
causelessly sought.
5527. vantavar ellām cantaiyil kuṭiyā?
Do all that come to market reside there?
5528. vanta viṉai pōkātu vārā viṉai vārātu.
The evil that has betided one will not go, that which has not, will not
come.
5529. vanta aḷavilē ciṟukki pantaṭittāḷ, varavarac ciṟukki cōrntu pōṉāḷ.
The damsel played at ball as soon as she came, in the course of time she
became indolent.
5530. vantārai vāḻa vaikkum, maṇṇil piṟantārait tūṅka vaikkum.
It will cause strangers to flourish, and natives to decay and sink.
5531. vantālum cari, pōṉālum cari.
It is all the same whether he comes or goes.
5532. vantāṟ cummā varum varāmaṟpōṉāl oṉṟum varātu.
When good things come, they do so unsolicited; when they do not come,
not one of them appears.
5533. vampāṉa vārttai maṉatukku aruvaruppu.
Bad words are an abomination to the mind.
5534. vamput turaittaṉattāraik kumpiṭattakumō?
Is it proper to make obeisance to a government, that rules with
severity?
5535. vayatukkō naraittatu mayirukkō naraittatu.
Does the becoming grey arise from age or from the hair.
5536. vayal muyaṟciyil tāṉiyam uṇṭām.
The labour of the field brings grain.
5537. vayittiyaṉ talaimāṭṭil iruntu aḻutatupōla.
As the physician wept at the head of the bed.
5538. vayittiyaṉ kaiyaip pārttu vākkiṭṭatu pōla.
As the physician, after feeling the pulse, gave his opinion.
5539. vayittiyaṉ kaiviṭṭatu pōla.
As given up by a physician.
5540. vayittiyaṉukku mōkṣam illai, uvāttikku mōkṣa vaḻi uṇṭu.
A physician does notattain heaven, a teacher may.
5541. vayirākkiyattukku ampaṭṭak kattiyai viḻuṅkukiṟatā?
Will enthusiasm induce one to swallow a razor?
5542. vayiṟārap pōcaṉamum araiyāṟap puṭaivaiyum illai.
No food for the stomach, nor cloth to for the waist.
5543. vayiṟu nirampiṉāl pāṉai mūṭāḷ.
She will not cover the rice-pot if her belly is full.
5544. vayiṟṟaik kīṟik kāṇpittālum mā intira jālam eṉpār.
If one should cut open his bellyto prove his innocence, even then they
would attribute it to jugglery.
5545. vayiṟṟuc cōṟṟukkāka vayittīcuvaraṉ kōvil maṭṭum naṭappāṉ.
He will walk as far as Vaidiswaran kovil, if he can get a meal.
5546. vayiṟṟuk kuṭalaik kāṭṭiṉālum vāḻai nār eṉkiṟāṉ.
Although the entrails be shown to him, he says they are only the fibre
of the plantain tree.
5547. vayiṟṟup piḷḷaiyai nampi māṭu mēykkiṟa piḷḷaiyaip paṟi koṭuttāṟpōla.
As if one suffered herself to be deprived of her son, who was tending
cattle, in anticipation of a child in the womb.
5548. vayiṟṟup pāmpukkuk kaṭukum, vaḷaip pāmpukku vennīrum iṭu.
Take mustard to kill the maw-worm, and pour hot water to kill a snake in
a hole.
5549. varappō talaikku aṇai vāykkālō pañcu mettai.
The ridge of the field, his pillow; the channel, his cotton mattress.
5550.varampu uyara nīr uyarum, nīr uyara nel uyarum, nel uyarakkuṭi uyarum, kuṭi uyara
muṭi uyarum.
As the ridges rise the water will rise; as the water rises the paddy
will grow, as paddy flourishes, the population will increase; as that
increases the crown will rise.
5551. varampu uyarntāl nel uyarum, nel uyarntāl col uyarum.
When the fields are full of water, paddy will rise, when paddy rises the
farmer's authority will rise.
5552. varavara māmiyār kaḻutaipōl āṉāḷ.
In the course of time the mother-in-law became a donkey.
5553. varavukkut takunta celavu.
Expense according to one's income.
5554. varavu koñcam valippu metta.
The income small, the labour great.
5555. vari pōṭēl kēṭṭait tēṭēl.
Do not impose taxes, do not cause evil.
5556. varicaiyum illai, ariciyum illai.
Neither respect nor rice.
5557. varukiṟa pōtu ellām valiyum, varum, vantapiṉ pōkiṟapōtu ellām pōm.
When coming all comes unsought, and having come, all goes at once.
5558. varuṇaṉ cilarai vakuttup peyyumō?
Will clouds shower on a select few?
5559. varuntiṉāl varātatu oṉṟu illai.
Nothing is unattainable when steadily pursued.
5560. varunti varuntip pārttālum varukiṟa poḻututāṉ varum.
Though one labours never so hard, the desired good will only be obtained
in its time.
5561. varumuṉ kāppavaṉ coṉṉa puttiyai vantapiṉ kāppavaṉ taḷḷiṉatupōla.
As the after-wise, rejected the advice of the fore-sighted.
5562. varuvatu coṉṉēṉ paṭuvatu paṭu.
I predicted the consequence, suffer what you have to suffer.
5563. varuvatu vantatu eṉṟāl paṭuvatu paṭa vēṇṭum.
If that which was to come is come, endurance is necessary.
5564. varuvāṉ kuruṭaṉ viḻuvāṉ kiṇaṟṟilē.
The blind man will come and he will fall into the well.
5565. valimaikku vaḻakku illai.
Power admits of no disputes.
5566. valiyap peṇ koṭukkiṟōm eṉṟāl kulam eṉṉa kōttiram eṉṉa eṉpārkaḷ.
If a girl be offered in marriage unsolicited, they will enquire after
her family and after her tribe.
5667. valiya vanta cītēviyaik kālāl utaittut taḷḷalāmā?
Should the goddess of prosperity who came to you spontaneously, be
kicked and turned out ?
5568. valiyavaṉ eṭuttatu vaḻi.
The strong man's way is the way.
5569. valiya uṟavāṭi vācalilē vantālum, poy uṟavāṭip pōy vā eṉṟu collukiṟāṉ.
When I go to his door in a friendly way, he feigns friendship, but bids
me go away.
5570. valiyavaṉ veṭṭiṉatē vāykkāl .
That is the channel which was cut by the strongman.
5571. valaiyil cikkikkoṇṭa māṉ.
An ensnared deer.
5372. vallaṭi vaḻakkaic collaṭi māmi.
Mother-in-law, give an account of the violent quarrel.
5573. vallavaṉ āṭṭiya pamparam maṇalilum āṭum.
The top thrown by the strong will spin even in sand.
5574. vallamai pēcēl.
Boast not of thy power.
5575. vallavaṉukkup pullum āyutam.
Even a blade of grass is a weapon to the strong.
5576. vallavaṉukkup pul āyutam.
Grass is a weapon to the strong man.
5577. vallavaṉukku vallavaṉ vaiyakattil uṇṭu.
Every powerful man in the world has his rival.
5578. vallār iḷaittāl vantu iḷaippāṟum eṉṟu collātirārkaḷ cutta vīrarkaḷ.
When the powerful fail, the brave will surely welcome them.
5579. vallār koḷḷai vāḻaippaḻam ākum.
The spoils of the strong will soon go to waste as a plantain.
5580. valvilaik kūṟaiyum melvilaik kāḷaiyum ākātu.
A dear-bought cloth, and a low-priced bull are useless.
5581. vavvāl vīṭṭukku vavvāl vantāl nīyum toṅku nāṉum toṅku.
When one bat visits another, the host will say, you hang, and I will do
the same.
5582. vaḻikaṭṭip paṟikkiṟavaṉ tiruṭaṉ, varatarājalu ēṟukiṟatē karuṭaṉ.
He who waylays and plunders is a thief, the vehicle of Vishnu is the
hawk.
5583. vaḻinaṭai vārttai vākaṉam pōla.
Conversation on a journey is equal to a conveyance.
5584. vaḻiyilē kaṇṭa kutiraikku vaikkōṟ paḻutai kaṭivāḷam.
A straw rope is the bridle for a stray horse.
5585. vaḻiyilē kiṭakkiṟa kōṭāliyai eṭuttuk kāliṉ mēl pōṭṭukkoḷvāṉēṉ?
Why should a man meddle with a hatchet lying in the road and cut his
foot?
5586. vaḻiyē ēṟuka vaḻiyē mīḷuka.
Go by the way, and return by the way.
5587. vaḻiyē pōy vaḻiyē vantāl atikāri ceṅkōl eṉṉa ceyyum?
If a man go and come by the highway, what can the sceptre of the chief
do to him?
5588.vaḻiyē pōkiṟa caṉiyaṉai vārattukku vāṅkiṉāṟpōla.
Like hiring Saturn whom one met on his way.
5589.vaḻi vaḻiyāyp pōkumpōtu viti vitiyāy varukiṟatu.
As each goes on his way, destiny accompanies him.
5590.vaḻukki viḻāta kutirai vaḷamāṉa kutirai.
The horse that does not stumble is the best horse.
5591.vaḻuvaḻutta uṟavaip pārkkilum vairam paṟṟiya pakai naṉṟu.
Inveterate hatred is better than fickle friendship.
5592. vaḷarum kāy piñcilē teriyum.
The future fruit may be known when it nits.
5593. vaḷartta kaṭā mārpiṟ pāyntatu pōla.
As the trained ram flew at the breast.
5594. vaḷartta nāy mukattaip pārkkiṟatu pōl
As a trained dog looks at the face.
5595. vaḷarppu vakkaṇai aṟiyātu.
A hanger on does not understand politeness.
5596. vaḷavaṉ āyiṉum aḷavu aṟintu aḷittu uṇ.
Though possessed of abundant wealth, be moderate in giving and eating.
5597. vaḷainta mūṅkil aracaṉ muṭimēl, vaḷaiyāta mūṅkil kaḻaik kūttar kāliṉ kīḻ.
A bent bamboo above the king's crown, a straight one under the feet of
pole-dancers.
5598. vaṟiyōrkku aḻaku vaṟumaiyiṟ kēṇmai.
Decency in adversity is commendable in the indigent.
5599. vaṟutta payaṟu muḷaikkumā?
Will parched peas germinate?
5600. vaṟumai kaṇṭavar vaiyattil anēkar.
There are many in the world who are accustomed to poverty.
5601. vaṟumai vantāl pattum paṟantu pōm.
When poverty comes, the ten vital airs will fly off.
5602. vaṟumaikku mūtēviyum celvattiṟkuc cītēviyum.
Adversity is attended by the goddess of misfortune, prosperity by the
goddess of fortune.
5603. vaṉāntarattu nuḻainarikaḷ iṭaiyarkaḷiṉ tīrkka virōtikaḷ.
The sly jackals of the wilds, are inveterate enemies to shepherds.
5604. vaṉcol vaṇakkattilum iṉcol vaṇaṅkāmai nalam.
Better is gentle resistance, than submission with rough words.
5605. vākaṉam uḷḷavaṉ naṭaikku añcāṉ, pāl uṇṭāṉ pantikku añcāṉ.
He who has a conveyance will not be afraid of moving, he who is fed on
milk will not care to attend a feast.
5606. vākai iḷampiñcu kaṇṭavarkaḷ illai, vākāṉa teṉṉampiḷḷai kaṇṭavarkaḷ illai.
None ever saw the tender fruit of the Mimosa flexuosa, nor a straight
young cocoanut tree.
5607. vākai iḷam piñcu kaṇṭavarkaḷ uṇṭō ?
Has any one seen the tender fruit of the Mimosa ?
5608. vākkiṉāl keṭṭa kaḻutaiyaip pōkkilē viṭuṅkaḷ.
Let the ass go that brought evil on itself by braying.
5609. vāṅkiṉa kaṭaṉaik koṭāta vallāḷakaṇṭaṉ.
A mighty cheat who never pays his debts.
5610. vācaṟpaṭi iṭṭu viṭikiṟatō makātēvar iṭṭu viṭikiṟatō?
Does the sun rise by the permission of the threshold, or by the
permission Mahadeva?
5611. vāṇāḷukku ēṟṟa vayiṟṟu ericcal.
Sorrow suited to the present life.
5612. vāṇāḷ uṭaiyāṉ valuppaṭṭuc cākāṉ.
He who is destined to live long, is not liable to die by accident.
5613. vāṇipam ceyyiṟ kāṇiyum kuṟi.
In matters of trade, note down the smallest fraction.
5614. vāṇiyakkaṭṭai vayirak kaṭṭai tēyattēyat tuṭaippak kaṭṭai.
The pestle of an oil-press is tough, when worn away it may become a
broom-stick.
5615. vāṇiyaṉ ācai kōṇiyum koḷḷātu.
A gunny bag will not hold the excessive desires of the oil merchant.
5616. vāṇiyarkaḷ āṭum cekkai vaḷaiya varum erutukaḷ pōla.
Like the oxen that go round the oil-man's press.
5617. vātattu iyalpu eṭēl.
Do not indulge in rheumatic humour.
5618. vātam keṭuttatu pāti vaṇṇāṉ keṭuttatu pāti.
It was destroyed partly by the wind, and partly by the washerman.
5619. vātam ūti aṟi, vētam ōti aṟi.
Learn alchemy by experiment, and the vedas by recitation.
5620. vāti kaṇṇukku maṭṭam eṭṭu māṟṟu.
To the eye of an alchemist, common gold appears as standard gold.
5621. vātukku āṭiṉa tēvaṭiyāḷ vayatu ceṉṟāl kaḻutai mēyppāḷ.
When a distinguished dancing girl becomes old, she may tend asses.
5622. vātumuṟ kūṟēl.
Do not begin a quarrel.
5623. vāyāṭi vārttai maṭṭillā ravai.
The words of a babbler are fine dust.
5624. vāyālē tiṉṟu vāyālē kakkum veḷavālaippōl
Like a bat that feeds and ejects by the mouth.
5625. vāyālē kēṭṭāl vāḻaikkāyp piñcum koṭāṉ, tāypuruṣaṉ vantāl tārōṭē koṇṭu
pōvāṉ.
Though you entreat, he will not give even an unripe plantain, but if
thekeeperof his mothercomes, he will produce a whole bunch.
5626. vāyālē kēṭṭāl vāḻaippiñcum koṭāṉ taṇṭittut kēṭṭāl tārōṭē koṭuppāṉ.
When entreated he will not give an unripe plantain, but when punished he
will give a whole bunch.
5627. vāyilē uṟavu maṉatilē pakai.
Friendship in the mouth, and hatred in the mind.
5628. vāyilē uṇṭu vaḻi.
The way is in one's mouth.
5629. vāyaip pārttu āḷai ēyttāṉ.
Seeing the man's inability to speak, he cheated him.
5630. vāy iruntāl vaṅkāḷam pōkalām.
If possessed of a mouth, one may find his way to Bengal.
5831. vāy illā viṭṭāl nāy koṇṭu pōyviṭum.
If you hadno mouth, a dog would carry you away.
5632. vāy illāviṭṭāl nāykūṭac caṭṭai ceyyātu.
If not possessedof a mouth, even a dog will not take notice of him.
5633. vāy iruntāl piḷḷai piḻaikkum.
If the child have a mouth, it will live.
5634. vāy uḷḷa peṭṭikkut tūr illai.
The box that has a mouth, has no bottom.
5635. vāy kāynta puli āḷ mēlē viḻuntatu pōla.
As a hungry tiger fell on a man.
5636. vāykkoḻuppuc cīlaiyil vaṭikiṟatu.
His arrogance oozes through his cloth.
5637. vāykku uṇṭō vātam?
Does rheumatism affect one's mouth?.
5638. vāy carkkarai kai kokkarai.
Sugarin his mouth, and the very opposite in the hand.
5639. vāy collum piṭari kumpiṭum.
The mouth will resist, the nape of the neck will worship.
5640. vāytāṉ irukkiṉṟatu vāykku aricikku vaḻi illai.
He has a mouth, but no means of procuring rice for it.
5641. vāy tiṟakkap poy tiṟakkum.
When the mouth opens, lies come forth.
5642. vāyt taviṭum pōy aṭuppu neruppum pōṉatu pōla.
Like losing bran from the mouth, and fire from the hearth.
5643. vāy nallatāṉāl ūr nallatu.
When the mouth is good, the village is good.
5644. vāy pārttavaḷ vāḻvu iḻantāḷ ampalam pārttavaṉ peṇṭu iḻantāṉ.
She who was looking at the mouth, became a widow : and he who watched
the house, lost his wife.
5645. vāy puḷittatō māṅkāy puḷittatō?
Which is sour, the mouth or the mango?
5646. vāy matattāl vaḻakku iḻantāṉ.
He lost his suit by the haughty words of his mouth.
5647. vāy matattāl vāḻvai iḻantāṉ.
He lost his fortune by the arrogance of his mouth.
5648. vāy vāḻaippaḻam kai karuṇaikkiḻaṅku.
Plantain fruit in the mouth, and karanai roots in the hands.
5649. vārttai iruntu pōm, vaḻi tūrntupōm.
Words will endure, ways will fall into disuse.
5650. vālipattilē mutirnta putti kuṟukiṉa vayatukku aṭaiyāḷam.
Premature genius foretokens a short life.
5651. vāl nīṇṭa karikkuruvi valam iruntu iṭam pōṉāl, kāl naṭaiyāyppōṉavarkaḷ kaṉaka
taṇṭikai ēṟuvārkaḷ.
If a long tailed blackbird fly from right to left, those who were on
foot will, on their return, mount palanquins wrought with gold.
5652. vāl pōṉālum pōkiṟatu eṉakkut tōl vēṇṭum.
It does not matter if the tail is lost, I want the skin.
5653. vāḻāta peṇṇukku mai ēṇṭi, poṭṭu ēṇṭi, mañcaḷ kuḷi ēṇṭi?
Of what use are paint, the marriage symbol and turmeric water, to a
woman who refuses to become a wife?
5654. vāḻukiṟa vīṭṭil maranāy kaṭṭiṉatupōla.
As a polecat was tied up in a house occupied by a prosperous family.
5655. vāḻum piḷḷaiyai viḷaiyāṭṭilē teriyum.
The future prosperity of a person may be known when he is playing as a
child.
5656. vāḻai aṭi vāḻai.
The sucker of a plantain tree becomes another tree.
5657. vāḻaippaḻattil ūci ēṟuvatu pōla.
Like a needle penetrating a plantain.
5658. vāḻaippaḻam tiṉṉāta kuraṅku illai.
There are no monkeys that will not eat plantain fruit.
5659. vāḻaippaḻam koṇṭupōṉavaḷ vācalil iruntāḷ, vāykoṇṭupōṉavaḷ naṭu vīṭṭil
iruntāḷ.
She that took the plantains sat at the entrance, she that took her
mouth, seated herself in the middle of the house.
The one who trusted her power of address obtained a better place than
the one who sought recognition by a gift.
5660. vāḻai vaṭakku īṉum vāṉ kamuku teṟku īṉum.
Plantain trees put forth their bunches to the north, and arica-nut
trees, on the south.
5661. vāḻkiṟa peṇṇait tāyār keṭuttatu pōla.
As a mother ruined a girl that was settled in life.
5662. vāḻkiṟa vīṭṭil vaṉkuraṅku vaittatu pōla.
Like placing a monkey in a family.
5663. vāḻkiṟa vīṭṭukku itu oru vaṉkuraṅku.
He is as a monkey in a house.
5664. vāḻkiṟa vīṭṭukku vāḻai vaittuppār.
Ascertain the future of a family by putting down plantain trees.
It is said that plantains naturally put forth their bunches to the
north. Should the fruit appear otherwise, it is considered ominous to
the homestead.
5665. vāḻkkai koṭuttavaṉ kaiyil vāṇāḷum.
Length of days is in the hands of him who gave prosperity.
5666. vāḻntatu keṭṭāl vaṟu ōṭṭukkum ākātu.
When that which flourished decays, it is not worth even a potsherd.
5667. vāḻntavaṉ vaṟiyavaṉāṉāl vaṟai ōṭṭiṟkum ākāṉ.
If a prosperous person-suffer reverses, he will not be worth a
blackpotsherd.
5668. vāḻnta makkaḷ vantāl vaṇṇat taṭukkuiṭu, keṭṭamakaḷ vantāl kiḻinta taṭukku iṭu.
When the daughter who lives in affluencepays a visit, let her be seated
on a fine mạt; when she who is reduced to poverty comes seat her on an
old mat.
5669. vāḻvārukkuc cītēvi vāyilē.
The goddess of fortune is in the mouth of the prosperous.
5670. vāḻvum cilatu kālam tāḻvum cilatu kālam.
Prosperity fora time, and adversity for a time.
5671. vāḷukku āyiram tōḷukku āyiram campātittālum maṭaṭāyc celaviṭu. Though
you may acquire thousands of wealth by dexterity, and physical strength,
be frugal.
5672. vāḷukku āyiram tōḷukku āyiram.
A thousand by his sword, a thousand by his arm.
5673. vāṉattukkuk kīḻ iruntu maḻaikkup payappaṭalāmā?
Living under the conopy of heaven, may we be afraid of rain?
5674. vāṉamum pūmiyum koḷḷātāṉ marattilum kallilum iruppāṉā?
Will he whom heaven and earth cannot contain,dwell in wood and stone?
5675. vāṉamē īṉṟatu pūmiyē tāṅkiṟṟu.
The heavens produced, and the earth sustained.
5676. vāṉam pārkkap pōyum iṭaiñcalā?
When you go to gaze at the heavens, do you find any impediment?
5677. vāṉam curakkat tāṉam ciṟakkum.
When it rains abundantly, liberality will abound.
5678. vāṉam curuṅkiṟ ṟāṉam curuṅkum.
When drought prevails charity fails.
5679. vāṉ ceyta utavikku vaiyakam eṉṉa ceyyum?
What can the world do in return for the favours of heaven?
5680. vicumpiṟ ṟuḷi vīḻiṉallāl pacumpuṟ ṟalai kāṇpatu aritu.
If the sky withholds rain, not a blade of grass can be seen.
5681. vicuvācak kokku naṭamāṭic cettatām.
It is said that a devout crane died from wandering about.
5682. vicuvācam iruntāl vēciyum piḻaippāḷ, nicam iruntāl nīcaṉum taḻaippāṉ.
If she be faithful, even a harlot will prosper; if he be honest, even
the low-born will flourish.
5683. vicuvācap pūṉai karuvāṭṭait tūkkikkoṇṭu pōkiṟatām.
It is said that a devout cat carried away the dried fish.
5684. viṣattaik kuṭikkap pālāmā ?
Will poison when drunk turn into milk?
5685. viṣattiṉ mēl viṣam, viṣam pōkkum.
Poison is the medicine of poison.
5686. viṣam tiṉṟāl kollum.
If poison be swallowed, it will kill.
5687. viṣam kuṭittavaṉ miḷaku nīr kuṭikka vēṇṭum.
He who has swallowed poison must take pepper water.
5688. viṣam kuṭittālum cākār vicuvācikaḷ.
Though they may take poison, the faithful will not die.
5689. viṣam peritō pāvam peritō?
Which is the more destructive, poison or sin?
5690. viṣam tīra vaittiyaṉ vēṇṭum, pāvam tīrat teyvam vēṇṭum.
A physician is necessary to counteract poison, and God, to remove sin.
5691. viṣṇu periyavar eṉṟu srīraṅkattil pārkkavēṇṭum.
You must go to Srirangam to understand that Vishnu is great.
5692. viṣṇuvaip peritu eṉpār srīraṅkattil, civaṉaip peritu eṉpār aruṇācalattil.
The inhabitants of Srirangam say that Vishnu is great, those of
Arunasalam, say that Siva is great.
5693. viṣṇuvē camastam eṉpār cilar, civaṉē peritu eṉpār cilar.
Some profess that Vishnu is all in all, while others maintain that Siva
is the greater of the two.
5694. viṭāccurattukku viṣṇukarantai.
Vishnukarantai-Sphoeranthus Indicus-is a specific remedy for fever.
5695. viṭāta maḻai peytāl paṭāta pāṭu paṭa vēṇṭum.
Should it rain unceasingly, intolerable suffering would follow.
5696. viṭāta maḻaiyāl illi oḻukku aṭaipaṭum.
Unceasing rain stops leaks.
5697. viṭintāl teriyum māppiḷḷai kuruṭum peṇ kuruṭum.
It will be known at day-break whether the bridegroom or the bride is
blind.
5698. viṭintum peṇṇukku muṭṭākkō?
Does the woman require a veil even after sunrise?
5699. viṭiya viṭiyak katai kēṭṭu irāmaṉukkuc cītai eṉṉa vēṇṭumeṉṟu kēṭṭatu pōla.
As one asked what relationship existed between Rama and Sita after
listening to their history till day-break.
5700. viṭiyaṟkālam kaliyāṇam, piṭi aṭā tāmpūlam.
The marriage will take place at dawn, thou fellow, take betel.
5701. viṭiyā mūñcikku vēlai akappaṭṭālum kūli akappaṭātu.
Though the unfortunate may find work, he will not get his hire.
5702. viṭiyumaṭṭum iṟaittavaṉum viṭintapiṉpu cālai uṭaittavaṉum cari.
He that draws water till day-break, and he that breaks his bucket at
day-break, are on an equality.
5703. viṭiyumaṭṭum maḻai peytālum ōṭṭāṅkiḷiñcil muḷaikkātu.
Though it may rain till day-break, a potsherd will not germinate.
5704. viṭṭiṟpūcciyaippōl paṟantu tirikiṟāṉ.
He flits about like a grass-hopper.
5705. viṭṭu viṭṭup peykiṟa maḻaiyilum viṭāmaṟ peykiṟa tuvāṉam nallatu.
Unceasing driving rain is preferable to intermitted showers.
5706. viṭṭutaṭā ācai viḷām paḻattu ōṭṭōṭē.
The pleasure of the wood-apple ceases with the shell.
5707. viṇ kāṭṭap pōṉavaṉ kaṇ kāṭṭa vantāṉām.
It is said that he who went to point out the heavens, returned to shew
his eyes.
5708. viṇṇāṇam eṅkē, kiṉṉaram eṅkē?
What is become of your ostentation, and where is your guitar?
5709. viṇṇumālaikkuk kaliyāṇam viḻuntu koṭṭaṭā cāmpuvā.
O, thou tomtom beater, Vinumal is to be married, fall down and beat your
tomtom.
5710. viṇṇēṟu tappiṉālum kaṇṇēṟu tappātu.
One may escape a thunderbolt, but he cannot escape the effects of an
evil eye.
5711. viṇtoṭu koṭi mūṭi mēruvum vīṟaḷi teṉticaik kiriyum.
Meru whose summit reaches to heaven, and the merit giving mountain on
the south.
5712. viṇ poyttāl maṇ poykkum.
If the sky fail, the earth will fail.
5713. viṇ valitō maṇ valitō?
Which is the more powerful heaven or earth ?
5714. viti pōkiṟa vaḻiyē mati pōkum.
The mind will follow destiny.
5715. viti muṭintavaṉaip pāmpu kaṭikkum.
A viper will bite him whose prescribed term of life is at an end.
5716. vitiyai matiyāl taṭukkalāmā?
Can destiny be averted by prescience?
5717. vistārakkāraṉ cettāl piḻaikkāṉ.
A boaster if he die, cannot return to life.
5718. vittu illāc campiratāyam mēlum illaik kīḻum illai.
Out of nothing, nothing comes, whether above or below.
5719. vittuvāṉukku etu paratēcam?
What country is foreign to a man of learning?
5720. vittuvāṉkaḷukku etu peritu?
What is difficult to the learned?
5721. vittaik kaḷḷi māmiyār viṟaku oṭikkap pōṉāḷām kaṟṟāḻai muḷ vantukottōṭē
taittatām.
The sapient old mother-in-law is said to have got foul of the thorny
cactus, when she went to gather firewood.
5722. vittai aṟṟavaṉ aḻaku vācaṉai illā murukkam pūppōla.
The beauty of the unlettered, is like the inodorous Muruku flower.
5723. vittai virumpu.
Desire learning.
5724. vittaik kaḷḷi, viḷaiyāṭṭuk kaḷḷi, pākaṟkāy viṟṟa paḻaṅkaḷḷi.
She is a hypocrite, and idle; she is that old thief that sold pagal
fruit.
5725. vittaiyaṭi māmi vikkutaṭi paṇikāram.
O thou pretending mother-in-law, cakes stick in my throat.
5726. vittaiyil eḷiyatu cūṉiyam, paṉṉattilē eḷiyatu nīṟṟup peṭṭi.
Of arts sorcery is the easiest, of textures the pastry boiling basket.
5727. vittai aṭikkiṟa kōḻikku vilāvil irukkiṟatām pittu.
It is said that an hypercritical hen has its bile in its chest.
5728. vittaiyuḷḷavaṉ periyavaṉ.
He who is learned, is truly great.
5729. vinācakālē viparīta putti.
Bent on destruction by a strange fatality.
5730. viyācciyam cēṟṟil naṭṭa kampam.
Litigation is a pole planted in mud.
5731. viyātikku maruntu uṇṭu vitikku maruntu uṇṭā ?
Medicine may be had for a disease, --is there any for destiny?
5732. viyātiyilum maruntu koṭitu.
The medicine is worse than the disease.
5733. viratattilum peritō oru canti?
Is fasting more meritorious than penance?
5734. viratam keṭṭālum cukam takkavēṇṭum.
Though the penance may prove ineffectual, one must have regard to his
comfort.
5735. viralukkut takka vīkkam.
The swelling will be proportioned to the size of the finger.
5736. viral utavi viruntiṉar utavār.
Guests are not as serviceable, as are one's fingers.
5737. viral ural āṉāl uṭal eṉṉa ākum?
If the finger swell to the size of a rice mortar, how large will the
mortar be when that swells?
5738. viral pōkāta iṭattil ural pōmā?
Can a mortar pass through an opening which is not large enough to admit
a finger?
5739. virinta ulakil terintavar cilar.
There are few on the face of the wide world who are wise.
5740. virutu kūṟi vantu ceṭiyil nuḻaiyalāmā?
Anxious for fame, may one crawl under bushes?
5741. virutukkō vēṭṭai āṭukiṟatu?
What, hunting, to acquire fame?
5742. viruttācalam pōṉāl tiraṭ pāvam pōkum.
Pilgrimage to Viruttachalam will expiate great sins.
5743. viruntu iṭṭup pakai tēṭukiṟatu.
Seeking enemies by means of hospitality.
5744. viruntilōrkku illaip poruntiya oḻukkam.
The inhospitable are destitute of agreeable manners.
5745. viruntu illāc cōṟu maruntu.
Food without hospitality is medicine.
5746. viruntaip paṇṇip poruntap paṇṇu.
Win your enemy by hospitality.
5747. viruntum maruntum mūṉṟu poḻutu.
Hospitality and medicine must be confined to three days.
5748. viruppattiṉāl ākātatu vīmpiṉāl ākumā?
Can that which is unattainable by ambition, be attained by mere
boasting?
5749. virai oṉṟu pōṭac curai oṉṟu muḷaikkumā?
Will the bottle gourd spring up when a different seed is sown?
5750. vilakkak kūṭāta tuṉpattiṟku vicaṉappaṭātē.
Do not fret about disagreeables that cannot be averted.
5751. vilaṅkai viṭṭut toḻuvil māṭṭikkoṇṭatu pōla.
Likebeing put in the stocks after liberation from chains.
5752. vilai makaṭku aḻaku taṉ mēṉi miṉukkutal.
It befits a harlot to make her person shine.
5753. vil aṭiyāl cākāttu kal aṭiyāl cākumā?
Will that which resisted the stroke of a bow, die by the pelting of
stones?
5754. vil illātavaṉ ampu tēṭuvāṉ ēṉ?
Why should a man without a bow seek arrows?
5755. villampō collampō?
Is it an arrow or sarcasm?
5756. villuk kuṉiyātu eytāl, vilakātu etirtta pakai.
If you shoot an arrow when the bow is not sufficiently bent, the enemy
will not retreat.
5757. villukku vijayaṉ parikku nakulaṉ.
In archery Vijaya, in horsemanship Nakula.
5758 vil vaḷaintāl mōcam tarum.
The bending of a bow is dangerous.
5759. vilvappaḻam tiṉpār pittam pōka, paṉampaḻam tiṉpār paci pōka.
They eat vilvam fruit to remove biliousness, and palmyrah fruit to
appease hunger.
5760. vivēkattiṉ mēṉmai avivēkattai oḻittal.
It is the excellence of discretion to avoid indiscretion.
5761. viḻalukku iṟaitta nīr.
Water drawn for coarse grass.
5762. viḻittavaṉ kaṉṟu nākukaṉṟu tūṅkiṉavaṉ kaṉṟu kaṭākkaṉṟu .
The calf of the man who watched is a female; that of him who slept is a
male.
5763. viḻukiṟa cuvarilē kai vaippāṉ ēṉ?
Why put your hand on a tottering wall ?
5764. viḻuṅkiṉa racam vayiṟṟil irātu.
Swallowed mercury will not remain in the stomach.
5765. viḻunta piḷḷaiyai eṭukka nēram illai.
No time to lift up the child that has fallen down.
5766. viḻuntālum mīcaiyil maṇ oṭṭavillai eṉkiṟāṉ.
Though he has fallen down, he says that his mustache is not soiled with
dust.
5767. viḷakkumāṟṟukkup paṭṭuk kuñcamā?
What, a silk tassel for a broom?
5768. viḷakkeṇṇeykkuk kēṭē tavirap piḷḷai piḻaippatu illai.
The child did not survive, — it was only waste of oil.
5769. viḷakkeṇṇeyait taṭavikkoṇṭu āṟṟu maṇalil puraṇṭālum oṭṭukiṟatu tāṉ oṭṭum.
Though one roll himself in sand, after applying oil to his body, he
cannot make a larger quantity of sand adhere to his body.
5770. viḷakkeṇṇeyām talaikku eṇṇey.
It is said that what he uses for the head is lamp-oil.
5771. viḷakkaip piṭittukkoṇṭu kiṇaṟṟil viḻukiṟatā?
What, falling into a well with a lighted lamp in the hand?
5772. viḷakkaik koḷuttik kīḻē vaippār uṇṭō ?
Does any one place a lighted lamp on the floor?
5773. viḷakkai vaittukkoṇṭu neruppukku alaikiṟatu pōla.
Like wandering abroad for fire, while there is a lighted lamp in the
house.
5774. viḷakku oḷikku ācaippaṭṭa viṭṭil pōla.
Like a grass-hopper fascinated by a lighted lamp.
5775. viḷaṅkā maṭaiyaṉ viṟakukkup pōṉāl viṟaku kiṭaittālum koṭi kiṭaiyātu.
If a simpleton go for firewood, though it be found, a creeper to bind it
into a bundle will not be found.
5776. viḷaiyāṭṭup piḷḷai viṣattukku añcātu.
A playful child will not fear venomous reptiles.
5777. viḷaiyāṭṭāy iruntatu viṉaiyāy muṭintatu.
Originating in playfulness it ended seriously.
5778. viḷaiyāṭṭup paṇṭam vīṭu vantu cērātu.
Things prepared by playful children never come home.
5779. viḷaiyum payir muḷaiyilē teriyum.
The future crop is known in the germ.
5780. viṟaku kaṭṭukkāraṉukku nārai valam āṉāl oru paṇam viṟkiṟatu oṉṟē kāṟpaṇam
viṟkum.
If a crane cross a firewood man from left to right, what he sells
ordinarily for a fanam, will fetch a fanam and a quarter.
If a crow fly on the right of one going out of his house, he is sure to
meet with success. If on the left, he will not obtain what be seeks.
5781. viṟaku kōṇalāṉālum neruppup paṟṟātā?
Will firewood not ignite, because crooked?
5782. viṟṟa kuṇṭaikkup pul pōṭuvāṉ ēṉ ?
Why feed a bullock after it is sold?
5783. viṉaikkālam varum kālam, maṉai vaḻiyum teriyātu.
When times are inauspicious, one does not know his way home.
5784. viṉai vitaittavaṉ viṉai aṟuppāṉ tiṉai vitaittavaṉ tiṉai aṟuppāṉ.
He who sows actions will reap actions, he that sows millet will reap
millet.
5785. vīkkamō tūkkamō?
Is it the effect of swelling or of sleep?
5786. vīkkam kaṇṭāl tūkkamām.
Swelling is followed by languor.
5787. vīṅkalukku viṣam atikam.
Looking at the swelling, the inflammation is excessive.
5788. vīcam iṟutta kuṭi nācam.
The payment of a sixteenth is a loss to a family.
5789. vīci naṭantāl veḷḷi vīcam kuṟaiyum.
Silver ornaments are injured by swinging the arms when walking.
5790. vīccu eṉṟālum viṭuvēṉā, vīra puttira ampalakārā?
Thou son of the violent Ambalakaran, shall I let thee go because thou
squeakest?
5791. vīṭu acaiyāmal tiṉṉum yāṉai acaintu tiṉṉum.
A house consumes standing still, an elephant when moving.
5792. vīṭu ellām kuruṭu, vācal ellām kiṇaṟu.
The inmates of the house are all blind, and there are wells all over the
yard.
5793. vīṭu kaṭṭukiṟatu aritu, vīṭu aḻikkiṟatu eḷitu.
To build a house is difficult, to destroy one is easy.
5794. vīṭu kaṭṭumuṉṉam kiṇaṟu veṭṭa vēṇṭum.
Before building a house dig a well.
In a Sanscrit work on architecture I saw some years ago, the advice here
expressed is given as the first thing to be attended to, when selecting
a site for a house.
5795. vīṭu taruvōṉ mēlum taruvōṉ.
He who gives a house may give more than that.
5796. vīṭu niṟainta viḷakkumāṟu.
A house full of brooms.
5797. vīṭu pō eṉkiṟatu, kāṭu vā eṉkiṟatu.
The homestead says go, the place of cremation says come.
5798. vīṭu veṟu vīṭu vēlūr atikāram.
His house is empty, but he acts as if he were the chief of Vellore.
One of the Nabob's palaces was at Vellore.
5799. vīṭu veṟuvīṭāy iruntālum maṇiyam ēḻu ūr.
Although his house is empty, he is the manager of seven villages.
5800. vīṭṭil aḻaku vēmpu aṭiyākum.
The shade of a margosa tree is good for a house.
5801. vīṭṭil aṭaṅkātavaṉ ūril aṭaṅkuvāṉ.
He who is not obedient in the house will obey in the village.
5802. vīṭṭukkarumam nāṭṭukku uraiyēl.
Do not make known abroad the affairs of the homestead.
5803. vīṭṭukku iruntāl veṇkalap peṇṭāṭṭi, vīṭṭukku illāmaṟ pōṉāl tūṅkaṟ
peṇṭāṭṭi.
If in the house she is bell-metal wife, if she goes abroad, she is a
slumbering wife.
5804. vīṭṭukku alaṅkāram periya kuṭi.
A large family gives beauty to a house.
5805. vīṭṭukkuc cōṟṟukku illai civaṉ aṟivāṉ, nāṭṭukkuc celvappiḷḷai nāṉ
allavō?
Siva knows that I have no rice at home, yet am I not regarded abroad asa
wealthy person?
5806. vīṭṭukku alaṅkāram viḷakku.
A lighted lamp gives beauty to a house.
5807. vīṭṭukku vīṭu maṇ aṭupputtāṉ.
An earthen hearth is the rule in all houses.
5808. vīṭṭukku alaṅkāram maṉaiyāḷ.
A wife gives beauty to a house.
5809. vīṭṭukku alaṅkāram vēḷāṇmai.
Grain gives beauty to a house.
5810. vīṭṭuc celvam māṭu, tōṭṭac celvam muruṅkaikkāy.
A cow is house-wealth, and murungai fruit is garden-wealth.
5811. vīṭṭuc cōṟṟait tiṉṟu vīṇ caṇṭaikkup pōvāṉēṉ?
Why go and quarrel causelessly when eating your own rice?
5812. vīṭṭuc cōṟṟaip pōṭṭu vīṇ pēccuk kēṭpāṉēṉ?
Having given his own rice why should one hear abuse?
5813. vīṭṭup pāmpu kāṭṭukkup pōṉāl atuvum kāṭṭup pāmpu ākum.
If a tamed snake go to the jungle, it will become a wild one.
5814. vīṭṭu vēlai veḷi vēlai pārttuk kāṭṭu vēlaikkuk kaṭṭōṭē pōkalām.
After finishing house work and out door work, one should go to that of
the jungle with due preparation.
5815. vīṭṭup piḷḷaiyum veḷip piḷḷaiyum vittiyācam aṟiyātu.
Children in one's own house and strange children make no difference.
5816. vīṭṭu mūtēviyum kāṭṭu mūtēviyum kūṭi ulāvukiṟatu.
The house-goddess of misfortune, and one of the wilds are walking
together.
5817. vīṭṭukkup pukaḻcciyō nāṭṭukkup pukaḻcciyō?
Is itfame to a family or to the country?
5818. vīṭṭukkāriyam pārātavaṉ nāṭṭuk kāriyam pārppāṉā?
Will he who cannot manage his own household affairs, attend to the
management of a country?
5819. vīṭṭukkāri eṉṟu peṇcātikkup peyar.
Another name for a wife is the mistress of the house.
5820. vīṭṭukku vīraṉ kāṭṭukkuk kaḷḷaṉ.
At home, a hero, abroad, a coward.
5821. vīṭṭukku vīṭu etir vīṭu ākātu.
The door-ways of opposite houses must not be over against each other.
5822. vīṭṭukku ēṟṟiṉa viḷakku viruntukkum ākum.
A lamp lit in a house for the inmates may answer for a feast.
5823. vīṭṭukku vāyttatu erumai, mēṭṭukku vāyttatu pōr.
A buffaloa makes a house prosper, a corn-stack makes high ground
conspicuous.
5824. vīṭṭukku oru vācaṟpaṭi, pūṭṭukku oru tiṟavukōl.
One step as an entrance to the house, and one key to a lock.
5825. vīṭṭup peṇcāti vēmpum kāṭṭup peṇcāti karumpum.
The wife is a margosa tree, the mistress sugar-cane.
5826. vīṭṭaik kaṭṭik kuraṅkaik kuṭi vaittatu pōla.
Like building a house, and allowing a monkey to dwell there in.
5827. vīṭṭaik kāttu aruḷ, pāṭṭaip pārttu aruḷ.
Watch your house, and manage your affairs.
5828. vīṭṭaik kaṭṭip pār, kaliyāṇam ceytu pār.
Enjoy the convenience of a house after building it, and the happiness of
the conjugal state, after marrying a wife.
5829. vīṭṭaip piṭuṅki viṟakāy erittālum vīṇātivīṇaṉukku aintu paṇam eppaṭi varum?
Although he may pull down his house and use it for firewood, the
notorious idler can never get five fanams?
5830. vīṭṭaik kātta nāyum, kāṭṭaik kātta nariyum, vīṇ pōkātu.
The expectations of a dog in a house, and a fox in the jungle are not
vain.
