5831. vīṭṭaik kaṭṭi ōṭṭaip pōṭu.
Build the house, and then tile it.
5832. vīṭṭaik kaṭṭu, allatu kāṭṭai aḻi.
Either build the house, or destroy the jungle.
5833. vīṭṭai ēṉ iṭittāy mūṭṭaippūccikkup payantu?
Why pull down the house, for fear of bugs ?
5834. vīṭṭai ellām vellattiṟku māṟiṉāṉ.
He gave the whole house in exchange for sugar.
5835. vīṇarukkuc ceytatu ellām vīṇām, kaṭaṟkaraiyil kāṇum maṇalmēl eḻuttukkāṇ.
Favours shewn to the worthless are vain, they are like writing on the
sand of the sea-shore.
5836. vīṇāy uṭainta caṭṭi vēṇṭiyatu uṇṭu eṉ talaiyiṟ pūṇāram pūṇṭa putumaitaṉaik
kaṇṭatu illai.
There are plenty of pans uselessly broken, I have scarcely ever seen my
head adorned with jewels.
5837. vīṇukku uḻaikkiṟavaṉ vīṇaṉ.
He who labours for no purpose is a fool.
5838. vīṇ iḻavukku mār aṭikkiṟatā?
Am I to smite my breast on account of the death of a stranger?
5839. vīṇ iḻavukku vīṭṭaik kaṭṭip pāra iḻaviṟkup pantal pōṭu.
Build a house for the use of a stranger, and put up a pandal for the
same purpose.
5840. vīṇ viparītattāl pētaiyar vīṇ celavu ceyya uṭaṉ paṭuvārkaḷ.
Fools are extravagant through mere perverseness.
5841. vīmpuk kuppaiyil viḷaiyum vīṇ ceṭi.
A useless shrub growing on a rubbish-heapof ostentation.
5842. vīmpukku vēṭam koḷḷātē.
Do not assume a garb for mere show.
5843. vīmpu pēcukiṟavaṉ aḻivāṉ, vīriyam pēcukiṟavaṉ viḻuvāṉ.
A boaster will be ruined, he who talks as a hero will fall.
5844. vīram pēcikkoṇṭu eḻunta cēvakaṉ veṭṭum kaḷam kaṇṭu mutukiṭalāmā?
May a soldier who defiantly rose to the combat, retreat in sight of the
battle field?
5845. vīraṉ kēṇmai kūr ampākum.
The friendship of a hero, is a sharp arrow.
5846. vīriyam peritō kāriyam peritō?
Which is the greater boasting or acting?
5847. veṅkaṇṇai vāṅkum uṉ kaṇṇaik koṭuttu.
Give your eyes in exchange for white-eyed fish.
5848. vekujaṉa vākkiyam karttavviyam.
The sayings of the many involve duty.
5849. veccēṉavukku aṉṟi veṇṇey urukumā ?
Can butter be melted without heat?
5850. veṭkam keṭṭālum keṭaṭṭum, toppaiyil iṭṭāl pōtum.
It matters little if his honour is at stake, it is enough if his paunch
be filled.
5851. vēṭkattāl olkātavaḷ kulastirikkup pōkātu.
She who is not restrained by modesty, is not a woman of superiorbirth.
5852. veṭkam aṟṟa peṇpiḷḷai vīṇ.
A woman without shame is worthless.
5853. veṭkappaṭukiṟa vēciyum veṭkam keṭṭa camucāriyum utavātavarkaḷ.
Shame in a prostitute, and want of modesty in a wife, are equally out of
place.
5854. veṭkamē keṭṭu veḷippaṭṭa muṇṭaikku mukkāṭu ētu?
What signifies a veil to a widow who goes about without shame?
5855. veṭkattukku añciṉavaṉ caccaravu ceyvāṉā?
Will he who is afraid of disgrace quarrel?
5856. veṭkattukku añciṉavaṉ kaṭaṉukku añcuvāṉ.
He that is afraid of disgrace is afraid of running into debt.
5857. veṭkattai viṟṟuk kakkattilē koṇṭu pōkiṟāṉ.
Having sold his sense of shame, he carries its price under his arm.
5858. veṭkam keṭṭavaṉukku mēṉi ellām aḻukkuttāṉ.
The whole body of the shameless, is dirty.
5859. veṭṭap palam illai veṭṭikkup pōka maṉam illai.
He is too weak to cut, and unwilling to go out.
5860. veṭṭa veḷiyilē vaiyāḷi viṭukiṟatā?
Would you let loose a frisky calf in an open plain?
5861. veṭṭikku iṟaittu viḻalukkut taṇṇīr kaṭṭiṉatu pōla.
Like irrigating for no purpose.
5862. veṭṭik koṇṭuvā eṉṟāṟ kuttik koṇṭuvarukiṟāṉ.
When he is told to reap and bring, he pounds and brings.
5863. veṭṭivēril viciṟiyum, vilāmiccai vēril taṭṭiyum paṇṇu .
Make a fan of the cuscus root, and a tat of sweet scented grass root.
5864. veṭṭiṉa iṭattil calam ūṟum, vīṭu kaṭṭiṉa iṭattil naṭai ēṟum.
Where the ground is dug water will spring, where a house is built there
will be an entrance.
5865. veṭṭi ellām taṇṇīr, maṇ kaṭṭi ellām pul nāṟṟu
There is water all over the path, young grass all over the sod.
5866. veṭṭi veṭṭip pārttālum muṭṭak karikkācutāṉ akappaṭum.
Though you may examine by continued digging, you will get only coin of
coal in abundance.
5867. veṭṭikkup peṟṟu vēliyil eṟintārkaḷā?
Did they give birth to me in vain, and throw me at a hedge?
5868. veṭṭiyāṉum piṇaḻum kaṭṭikkoṇṭu aḻaṭṭum.
Let the Vettiyan and the corpse embrace each other, and weep.
5869. veṭṭiṉ kuḷattilum taṇṇīr kuṭiyātu kaṭṭiṉa vēliyum tāṇṭamāṭṭātu.
It neither drinks in an artificial reservoir, nor leaps an artificial
hedge.
5870. veṭṭiṉavaṉukku oru kēṇi vīṇāti vīṇaṉukkup pala kēṇi.
He that has sunk a public well has only one, a notorious idler has many.
5871. veṭṭu oṉṟu kaṇṭam iraṇṭu.
One cut, two slices.
5872. veṭṭeṉa uraiyē tuṭṭarkaḷ aṟaivar.
The wicked speak harshly.
5873. veṭṭeṉap pēcēl.
Do not speak harshly.
5874. veṇkalak kaṭaiyil yāṉai pukuntatu pōla.
As an elephant entered a brass vessel bazaar.
5875. veṇṭalaik karuṭaṉ vantiṭumāṉāl evar kaipporuḷum taṉ poruḷ ākum.
When a brahmany kite crosses one from right to left, he may become
possessed of the wealth of all.
5876. veṇṇeyyai vaittukkoṇṭu neykku aḻalāmā?
Having butter, would you weep for ghee?
5877. veṇṇey tiraḷukiṟapōtu tāḻi uṭaintāṟpōla.
As the churn broke just as the butter was forming.
5878. veṇṇey urukkukiṟataṟkuḷḷē peṇṇai āṟṟilē taṇṇīr varukiṟatu.
Ere butter can melt, freshesappear in the Pennar.
5879. venta cōṟṟait tiṉṟu vantatu ellām pitaṟṟukiṟāṉ.
He eats his rice, and talks at random.
5880. venta cōṟṟait tiṉṟu viti vantāl cākiṟatu.
Eating one's rice, and dying when the time comes.
5881. ventatu pōtum muṉṟāṉaiyilē koṭṭu.
It is boiled enough, put it into the end of my cloths.
5882. ventataip pōṭu muṉṟāṉaiyilē.
Put what is boiled into my cloth.
5883. venta puṇ viṉai ceyyātu.
No evil consequences can arise from a burn.
5884. ventayam iṭṭa kaṟikku cantēkam illai.
A curry with vendayam in it needs not be questioned.
5885. ventayam pōṭāta kaṟiyum kaṟi alla, cantai illāta ūrum ūr alla.
A curry made without vendayam-Trigonella Foenum Groecum-is not a curry,
a village without a market is not a village.
5886. ventāl teriyum veṅkāyam maṇappu.
The flavour of an onion will be known after it is boiled.
5887. vennīrilē vīṭu vēkumā?
Can a house be burnt down with hot water?
5888. veyyililē pōṭṭālum ularāṉ, taṇṇīrilē pōṭṭālum naṉaiyāṉ.
He will not dry in the Sunshine, nor dissolve in water.
5889. veyyōṉ veyil muṉ eri tīpam pōla.
Like a burning lamp in sunshine.
5890. vellap paṭai illai, tiṉṉap paṭai uṇṭu.
No army to conquer, though there are enough to consume.
5891. vellap pāṉaiyai eṟumpu moyttatu pōla.
As the ants swarmed on a pot of sugar.
5892. vellap pāṉaiyai īkkaḷ moyttukkoṇṭatu pōla.
As the flies swarmed on a pot of sugar.
5893. vellam cāppiṭukiṟavaṉ oruvaṉ viralaic cūppukiṟavaṉ oruvaṉ.
One eats the sugar, another links his fingers.
5894. vellap pōṉāṉ oru cellappiḷḷai mellap pōṉāṉ oru amuṅkut talaiyaṉ.
A pet child went to conquer, a flat-headed fellow followed him slowly.
5895. vellātu uṅkaḷ paṭai cellātu pāṇṭiyaṉ muṉ.
Your army will not conquer, nor even oppose Pandya.
5896. veḷiccam iruḷ āṉāl iruḷ eṉṉavām, meyññāṉi turmārkkaṉāṉāl aññāṉi eppaṭi
āvāṉ?
If light become darkness, what will the darkness be; if a wise man
becomes wicked, what a great fool he will be?
5897. veḷiccīr uṭcīraik kāṭṭum kaṇṇāṭi.
The exterior is the mirror of the interior.
5898. veḷuttatu ellām pāl kaṟuttatu ellām taṇṇīr.
Every thing white is milk, and every thing black is water.
5899. veḷuttu viṭṭālum cari cummā viṭṭālum cari.
It will be all the same whether he is punishedor let go.
5900. veḷḷam paḷḷattai nāṭum viti puttiyai nāṭum.
The flood inclines to a hollow, fate follows the intention.
5901. veḷḷam varuvataṟkumuṉ aṇai kōlik koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
One should raise the dam before the flood comes.
5902. veḷḷarikkāy viṟṟa paṭṭaṇam.
The city where cucumbers were sold.
5903. veḷḷāṭu naṉaikiṟatu eṉṟu vēṅkaippuli viḻuntu viḻuntu aḻukiṟatām.
It is said that the tiger is fallen down and crying for grief because
the goat was wet.
5904. veḷḷāṭu kuḻai tiṉṟatu pōl.
As the goat crops leaves.
5905. veḷḷāṭṭikkuc caṉṉatam vantāl viḻuntu viḻuntu kumpiṭa vēṇṭum.
If a servant girl, be possessed of a devil, all must fall down before
her.
5906. veḷḷāṭṭi peṟṟa piḷḷai viṭiyaṟkālam cettup pōyiṟṟu.
The child brought forth by a servant girl, died at sunrise.
5907. veḷḷāṭṭiyum peṇṭāṭṭiyum cariyā?
Are a maid servant and a wife, on an equality?
5908. veḷḷāḷaṉ marapē marapu kaḷḷar tiruṭṭē tiruṭṭu.
Of all ranks that of the Vellala is foremost, of thefts that of the
Kallar is notorious.
5909. veḷḷāḷaṉ keṭukkāviṭṭālum veḷḷōlai keṭukkum.
If the Vellala did not procure your ruin, his white olas will destroy
you.
5910. veḷḷāḷaṉ miṉukkup paṇṇik keṭṭāṉ, vēcicaḷukkup paṇṇik keṭṭāḷ.
The Vellala was ruined by adornment, the harlot by finery.
5911. veḷḷāḷaṉ kirantamum pārppāṉ tamiḻum viḻal viḻalē.
The Sanscrit of a Vellala, and the Tamil of a brahman, are equally
faulty.
5912. veḷḷāḷar ceyyāta vēḷāṇmai vēḷāṇmai alla.
Agriculture not performed by Vellalas, is no agriculture.
5913. veḷḷāḷar kuṭikku oru kaḷḷāḷar kuṭi.
A Kallala family in a Vellala house.
5914. veḷḷikku etirē pōṉālum, veḷḷāḷaṉukku etirē pōkalākātu.
One may go before an evil star, but not before a Vellalan.
5915. veḷḷikkup pōṭṭatum koḷḷikkup pōṭṭatum cari.
Money laid out on silver ornaments, and that spent for firewood are
equally a loss.
5916. veḷḷi pōṭṭa kālukku veṟuṅkāl aṭimaiyā?
Is a naked foot, the slave of a foot wearing silver ornaments?
5917. veḷḷi vaṭṭilum vēṇṭum viḷimpilē poṉṉum vēṇaṭum.
I must have a silver platter with a rim of gold.
5918. veḷḷerukkukkum veḷḷāṭṭup pālukkum kaḷḷak karu mēkam kāṇāmaṟ pōm.
The disease which blackens the skin, will be thoroughly cured by the
white species of erukku and goats milk.
5919. veḷḷai koṭukka viṉai tīrum.
One's sins will be expiated by giving cloth to the destitute.
5920. veḷḷaikkuk kaḷḷam illai.
White is faultless.
5921. veḷḷaikku illai kaḷḷac cintai .
The out-spoken are free from deceit.
5922. veṟuṅkait taṭṭāṉ irumpu ūtic cettāṉ.
A money-less goldsmith died by blowing iron.
5923. veṟikāraṉ kuṭiyai viṭṭāṉ vīṇ puttikkāraṉ pāvattukku añcāṉ.
A drunkard will not forsake drinking, a fool is not afraid of evil
deeds.
5924. veṟikoṇṭa yāṉai mitantu tirikiṟāppōlē.
As an elephant in rut wanders about unrestrained.
5925. veṟikkiṭṭu aḻiyēl.
Squander not by drinking.
5926. veṟu nāy cantaikkup pōṉāl veḷḷikkōlāl aṭipaṭṭu varum.
A dog which goes alone to the market, will be beaten with the steel -
yard.
5927. veṟum kai muḻam pōṭumā?
Can the arm measure a cubit, when there is nothing to be measured?
5928. veṟum caṭṭi tāḷikkiṟāṉ.
He flavours an empty chatty.
5929. veṟum puḷi tiṉṟāl paṟ kūcum.
Tamarinds if eaten alone, will set the teeth on edge.
5930. veṟum pāṉaiyil ī pukuntatupōla.
As flies entered an empty pot.
5931. veṟum pallukku ēṟṟa vīṟāppu.
Arrogance suited to one's worthlessness.
5932. veṟumpilukku vaṇṇāṉ māṟṟu.
Finery borrowed from a washerman.
5933. veṟuvāykku ilaikeṭṭavaṉ viṟakukkup pōṉāl viṟaku kiṭaittāṟ koṭi kiṭaiyātu
koṭi kiṭaittāl viṟaku kiṭaiyātu.
If a worthless fool go out to gather firewood, he cannot procure a
creeper, when he gets a creeper, he can find no firewood.
5934. veṟuvāy mellukiṟa ammaiyārukku nāḻi aval akappaṭṭatu pōla.
As a measure of bruised dried rice given to an old dame, who had nothing
to chew but an empty mouth.
5935. veṟu vāyait tiṉṟavaṉukku veḷḷai aval kiṭaittatu pōla.
As one who had nothing to eat but an empty mouth obtained bruised rice.
5936. veṟṟi peṟṟavaṉ cutta vīraṉ.
He who has conquered is a perfect hero.
5937. veṟṟilaikkut taṇṇīrum vēcaikku mañcaḷum pōla.
Like water to the betel creeper, and saffron to a prostitute.
5938. veṟṟilai pōl irukkum mikunta maram āki viṭum putti uḷḷa pūmarattukkup
pūviraṇṭu vakkaṇaiyām.
Its leaf is like betel leaf, it grows to a large tree, this intelligent
tree has two flowers.
5939. vēkattil nālu vitam uṇṭu.
Of speed there are four kinds.
5940. vēkap poṟuttatu āṟap poṟukkak kūṭātā?
Having been patient till the food was boiled, can you not wait till it
cools?
5941. vēkāta vīṭṭil vēkum kaṭṭai kāmam.
Lust is a burning block of wood in a house, not on fire.
5942. vēkāta cōṟṟukku viruntāḷikaḷ iraṇṭu peyar.
There are two guests for the insufficiently boiled rice.
5943. vēkiṟa vīṭṭil piṭuṅkukiṟatu lāpam.
Whatever is snatched from a burning house is an advantage.
5944. vēkiṟa vīṭṭukku veṭṭukiṟa kiṇaṟu.
To dig a well to put out a house on fire.
5945. vēkiṟa vīṭṭiṟkuk kaṇakkup pārppār uṇṭō?
Are there any who waste time in casting up the cost of a house when it
is on fire?
5946. vēkiṟa vīṭṭai avikkāmal iruppār uṇṭō?
Will not men extinguish the fire, when a house is on a blaze?
5947. vēci uṟavu kācilum paṇattiluntāṉ.
The friendship of a prostitute is in the money she gets.
5948. vēci kācu paṟippāḷ.
A prostitute knows how to deprive one of his money.
5949. vēciyarum nāyum viti nūl vaittiyarum pācam aṟṟu niṟpatu kaṇpār.
See how harlots, dogs, and physicians, are at variance.
5950. vēṭakkāraṉukkum āṭakkāraṉukkum pakai, vēcikkum tācikkum pakai.
Enmity exists among dancing masters and among harlots.
5951. vēṭakkārā vēṭam viṭaṭā, ōṭakkārā ōṭam viṭaṭā.
Thou hypocrite, quit thine hypocrisy, thou boatman, steer the boat.
5952. vēṭattil nālu vitam uṇṭu.
Of disguises there are four kinds.
5953. vēṭattiṉāl eṉṉa, veṇṇīṟṟiṉāl eṉṉa?
What avail special forms, what avails white ashes?
5954. vēṭamō tavavēṭam maṉatilō avavēṭam.
In appearance an ascetic, at heart a cheat.
5955. vēṭam mūṉṟu vakai.
There are three forms of disguise.
5956. vēṭam aḻintupōm.
Disguises will perish.
5957. vēṭam kūṭṭamum koḷḷātu.
His pretence is such that a room cannot contain it.
5938. vēṭarukku arumaiyāṉa vēṭṭai mucal vēṭṭai.
Foresters' favourite sport is hare hunting.
5959. vēṭarukku tēṉ pañcamā, mūṭarukku aṭi pañcamā?
Is honey rare among foresters, or chastisement to fools?
5960. vēṭar illā ūril yāvum kuṭi irukkum.
In a village where there are no hunters, all kinds of beasts may be
found.
5961. vēṭarkaḷil malaivēṭar vicēṣam.
Of hunters those that inhabit hill tracts, are the most distinguished.
5962. vēṭar kaiyil akappaṭṭa tēṉ kūṇṭupōl.
As a bee-hive in the hands a forester.
5963. vēṭṭai āṭic ciṅkam tiṉṉum.
The lion lives by hunting.
5964. vēṭṭaiyil periya vēṭṭai paṉṟi vēṭṭai.
Hog-hunting is the most exciting of sports.
5965. vēṭṭaiyil piriyamāṉa vēṭṭai cikkāri vēṭṭai.
The most favourite of sports is that of the huntsman.
5966. vēṇṭāta peṇṭāṭṭiyiṉ kaipaṭṭāṟ kuṟṟam kāl paṭṭāṟ kuṟṟam.
A wife not liked offends, whether she touches with the hand, or with the
foot.
5967. vēṇṭāta pērukku īntu eṉṉa , vēlaiyil āṟṟuttaṇṇīr viḻuntu eṉṉa?
What is the good of giving to those who are not in want, and what is the
good of a river flowing where work is being done?
5968. vēṇṭi viṉai ceyēl.
Do not act from selfish motives.
5969. vēṇṭi vēṇṭik koṭuttālum vēṇṭām eṉṟāṟpōla.
Like refusing to accept a thing when requested again and again.
5970. vēṇṭumeṉṟāl vīṭu vēṇṭām eṉṟāl kāṭu.
Domestic or ascetic life just as he pleases.
5971. vēṇṭum eṉṟu nūṟṟāl veṇṇeyk koṭipōla.
If one spins for her own benefit, the thread is as smooth as butter.
5972. vētattil nālu vitam uṇṭu.
Of Vedas there are four kinds.
5973. vētattiṟkum vikkirakapattikkum pakai.
The Vedas are opposed to idolatry.
5974. vētattai aṟiyāta kiḻavaṉ vīṇ.
The old man that knows not the Vedas; is worthless.
5975. vētattiṟku ulakam pakai ulakattiṟku ñāṉam pakai.
The world is at enmity with the Vedas, and wisdom with the world.
5976. vētam āyntu ōtal pōtakar muṟaimai.
It is the duty of religious teachers to study the Vedas before teaching
them.
5977. vētam poyttālum viyāḻam poyyātu.
Though the Vedas may fail, Jupiter will never fail.
5978. vētam kēṭṭavarai vētam kēṭṭavar eṉpāṉ ēṉ?
Why call those Vediar, who have heard the Vedas ?
5979. vētam ēṉ nātam ēṉ vistārak kaḷḷarukku?
What need has a widely known hypocrite of the Vedas or the special forms
of worship?
5980. vētam otta mittiraṉ.
A friend whose conduct is consistent with the precepts of the Vedas.
5981. vētam ōtiya vētiyarkku ōr maḻai.
One rain (in a month) for the brahmans that expound the Vedas.
5982. vētāraṇiyattil pāmpu kaṭikkiṟatum illai, vētāraṇiyattil pāmpu kuṟaikiṟatum
illai.
At Vedaranyam snakes do not bite, nor do they cease to abound.
5983. vētiyarkku aḻaku vētamum oḻukkamum.
The Vedas and virtue, form the beauty of the brahmans.
5984. vētiyarkku aḻaku vētam ōtutal.
It is the office of brahmans to expound the Vedas.
5985. vēntaṉum pāmpum cari.
A king and a snake are alike.
5986. vēntaṉ cīṟil āntuṇai illai.
No help if the king is angry.
5987. vēppeṇṇeyum āpattukku utavum.
Even margosa oil will avail on an emergency.
5988. vēppeṇṇey viṟṟa kācu kacakkumā?
Will the money obtained by the sale of margosa oil be bitter?
5989. vēppeṇṇey viruntu eṇṇey alla, maruntu eṇṇey.
Margosa oil is not used in entertainments; it is medicinal.
5990. vēmpil tēṉai viṭṭāl kacappu nīṅkumā?
Will the bitterness of margosa be removed by infusing honey into it?
5991. vēmpum karumpāccē veṟṟilaiyum nañcāccē.
The margosa has become sugar-cane, and betel has become poison.
5992. vēmpukkup pal aḻaku, vēlukkup pal iṟukum.
When cleaned with a margosa stick the teeth look beautiful, when cleaned
with a vel stick-Acacia arabica-they are made firm.
5993. vēmpum cari vēntaṉum cari.
The margosa and a king are alikę.
5994. vēmpum cari pāmpum cari.
A margosa tree and a snake are alike.
5995. vēmpai virumpa virumpak karumpu.
Constant use makes even margosa sweet.
5996. vēraik kalli vennīr vārtta katai.
The story of digging up a root; and pouring out hot water.
5997. vēr kaḷainta maram piḻaippatu eṅkē?
How can a tree deprived of its roots live?
5998. vēr niṉṟāl maram niṟkum, viyāpāram niṉṟāl ceṭṭi niṟpāṉ.
Trees endure as long as their roots are undecayed, merchants will
maintain their social position if their trade continues.
5999. vēr mūlikai, maramūlikai , kāy mūlikai.
Roots, wood, and fruit are all drugs.
6000. vēlamarattu muḷḷum ālamarattuk kaṉiyum āṉēṉ.
I am become a thorn of the vel, -Acacia arabica-and a fruit of the
banyan.
6001. vēlamarattiṟku niḻal illai, veḷḷāḷaṉukku uṟavu illai.
The vel tree casts no shade, the Vellala has no friendships.
6002. vēlam paṭṭai mēkattai nīkkum, ālam paṭṭai pittattai aṭikkum.
The bark of the vel cures venereal heat, that of the banyan remove bile.
6003. vēli oṉṟukkup paṉṉiraṇṭu kalam viraippāṭu.
Twelve kalams of seed corn for one veli of land.
A veli is nearly equal to five acres.
6004. vēli oṉṟukkup īraṇai māṭum, iraṇṭu āḷum vēṇṭum.
For every veli two yokes of oxen and two ploughmen are required.
6005. vēlikku ōṇāṉ cāṭci, ventatukkuc cokkaṉ cāṭci.
The chamelion is the witness of the hedge, the cook boy will testify to
the food being well boiled.
6006. vēlikkup pōṭṭa muḷ kālukku viṉaiyāccutu.
The thorns collected for a hedge, have proved injurious to the feet.
6007. vēli payirai mēyntāl viḷaivatu eppaṭi?
If the hedge consume the crop, how is the harvest to be obtained?
6008. vēlilum nālu palaṉ uṇṭu.
Even a vel tree is useful in four ways.
6009. vēlivaittuk kāppāṟṟāta kaṉṟum ālaivaittu āṭṭāta vāṇiyaṉum cari alla.
Plants not protected by a hedge, and an oil-monger who does not work a
press, are out of place.
6010. vēlai aṟṟa ampaṭṭaṉ pūṉaiyaip piṭittuc ciraittāṉām.
It is said that a barber who had nothing to do, got hold of a cat and
shaved it.
6011. vēlai atikam campaḷam koñcam.
The labour is excessive, the pay little.
6012. vēlai illā ūrukku rājā ēṉ, pāmpu illā ūrukkuk kīrippiḷḷai ēṉ?
What need is there of a king in a country where there is no work, or of
a mongoose where there are no snakes?
6013. vēlai illātavaṉukkuc cāppāṭu eṉṉattiṟku, eccicōṟṟṟuk kāraṉukku ṭampam
eṉṉattiṟku?
Why food to him who does no work, why display to him who lives on offal?
6014. vēlai illāta ampaṭṭaṉ āṭṭaic ciraittāṉām.
It is said that a barber who had no work, shaved a sheep.
6015. vēlai ēṉ, piḷḷai ēṉ, vēlai illārukkuc cāppāṭu ēṉ?
Why work, why child, why food to those who are destitute of energy?
6016. vēlaikkaḷḷikkup piḷḷai mēl cākku, veṭkam keṭṭa nāṟikku akamuṭaiyāṉ mēlē
cākku.
An idle woman pleads her child in excuse for her faults, a shameless
woman, her husband.
6017. vēlaikkaḷḷikku vēḷaikkuk kāṟ paṭi , vīṇ kaṭṭaikku vēḷaikku araip paṭi.
Toa shuffling woman a quarter measure for a meal, to a worthless block
half a measure.
6018. vēlaikkaḷḷikkup piḷḷai mēlē cākku.
A lazy woman neglects her work, and lays the blame on her child.
6019. vēlaikkāriyāy vantavaḷ vīṭṭukkāriyāṉāl avaḷ atiṣṭam.
If a maid servant becomes the mistress of a house, it is her fortune.
6020. vēlaikkāri eṉṟu vēṇṭiya pērkaḷ kēṭṭārkaḷ, kuṭittuṉakkāri eṉṟu
koṭukkamāṭṭōm eṉṟārkaḷ.
Many asked her in marriage thinking that she was skilful in domestic
affairs, but they refused on the plea that she was married girl.
6021. vēlaikkut takka kūli, viruppattukkut takka kūrmai.
Hire suited to the work, sharpness equal to the desires.
6022. vēlaikkō campaḷam, āḷukkō campaḷam?
Is the hire for the labour, or the labourer?
6023. vēlai ceytāṟ kūli, vēṣam pōṭṭāṟ kācu.
If the work be completed, hire; if the character has been sustained a
fee.
6024. vēlai ceyyāta piḷḷaiyaik kaiyil vai , vēlai ceykiṟa piḷḷaiyaik kālil vai.
Carry in arms, the child that does nowork, and the child which works,
place on your legs.
6025. vēlai miṉakkeṭṭa ampaṭṭaṉ peṇṭāṭṭi talaiyaic ciraittāṉām. Being without
work, thebarber is said to have shaved his wife’s head.
6026. vēlai miṉakkeṭṭu ampaṭṭaṉ pūṉaikkuṭṭiyaic ciraittāṉām.
It is said that a barber wasted his time by shaving a kitten.
6027. vēlai muttō piḷḷai muttō ?
Is the work a pearl, or the child a pearl?
6028. vēlaiyaip pārttuk kūli koṭu.
Look at the work before you pay the hire.
6029. vēlaiyaip pārttup peṇṇai eṭu, cālaiyaip pārttu ūrukku naṭa.
After ascertaining her domestic skill take a girl in marriage, proceed
to your village taking care not to miss the road.
6030. vēl vaittup payir ākkuvōr illai.
No one cultivates the vel-Acacia arabica-tree.
6031. vēḻattai otta viṉai vantāl tīrvatu eppaṭi?
When a monstrous evil betides one, how can it be removed?
6032. vēḻattiṟkuc ciṟitum peritāyt tōṉṟum.
To an elephant small objects appear great.
6033. vēḻam muḻaṅkiṉāṟpōl.
Like the thundering of an elephant:
6034. vēḷai aṟintu pēcu, nāḷai aṟintu payaṇam paṇṇu.
Speak opportunely, set out on a journey on an auspicious day.
6035. vēḷaikku araik kācu āyiram poṉ ākum.
Half acash at a time will, in the long run, amount to thousands of gold.
6036. vēḷaikku utavāta piḷḷai tāḻaṅkāykkuc cari.
A child who does not help in an emergency is like the fruit of the
screw-pine.
6037. vēḷaiyō avavēḷai vīṭṭilō aṉṉam illai.
The time is unfavourable, at home there is no rice.
6038. vēṟē viṉai tēvai illai, viṉaiyāttāḷkōvilukkuppōka vēṇṭiyatillai.
There is no need of another evil, it is not necessary to go to the
temple of the goddess of misfortune.
6039. vēṉalukkuk kaṉa maḻai varum, vēntaṉukkuk kaṉa caṉam cērum.
Excess of heat foretokens a heavy fall of rain, the presence of an king
attracts a multitude of people.
6040. vēṉiṟ kālattiṟku viciṟi āṉa kālattiṟku āccāvum tēkkum.
In the hot season a fan is useful, in prosperous times acha-ebony and
teak wood.
6041. vai eṉṟa eḻuttē peyarum viṉaiyum ākum.
The letter vai is both a noun and a verb.
6042. vaikaṟait tuyil eḻu.
Rise at dawn.
6043. vaikāci mātam āṟṟil taṇṇīr.
The river is in flood in May.
6044. vaikuṇṭam eṉpatu tiru mā nakaram.
That which is called Vaikundam is a very great city.
6945. vaikai āṟṟut taṇṇīr vēkam atikam.
The current in the Vaigai is great.
6046. vaikai āṟṟu veḷḷattil pālam nilaikkiṟatu illai.
When the Vaigai is in flood, its bridges do not escape uninjured.
6047. vaikai āṟu tāmiraparuṇikku mattimam.
The Vaigai issmaller than the Tamravarni.
6048. vaikkat teriyāmal vaikkōṟ pōrilē vaittāṉām.
It is said that he unwittingly placed it in a stack of straw.
6049. vaikkat teriyāmal vaittuviṭṭu vantavarai ellām kēṭkalāmā?
Having inserted it by mistake, may be ask every one that comes by to
help him out of the straits?
6050. vaikkōṟkaṭṭukkāraṉai oppukkuk kaṭṭi aḻutāppōla.
As one embraced a straw carrier and pretended to weep.
6051. vaikkōṟpaṭṭaṭaiyil kaṭṭiṉa nāy.
A dog tied by a stack of straw.
6052. vaikkōl pañcamā vaṟaṭ pacu pañcamā?
Which is the more scarce, straw or a barren cow?
6053. vaikkōl tiṉṉum kutirai vīṭṭuk kūraiyaiyum piṭuṅkum.
A horse which eats straw will also pull down thethatch of the house.
6054. vaikkōl tiṉkiṟa māṭṭukkup pāl koñcam, maturam atikam.
The cow that eats straw gives a small quantity of milk but it is very
sweet.
6055. vaikkōl tiṉkiṟa kutiraikku vēkam atikamā?
Is the horse that feeds on straw uncommonly fleet?
6056. vaikkōṟ kūraiyilum viḻaṟ kūrai veku nāḷ irukkum.
A reed roof lasts longer than a straw roof.
6057. vaikkōṟ kūḷamum oru vēḷaikku utavum.
Even old straw may be of use sometime or other.
6058. vaiciyarum cūttirarum iruntu allō pirāmaṇarum kṣattiriyarum varuvārkaḷ.
It is from Vaisyas and Sudras who must have existed previously, is it
not, that Brahmans and Kshatris must have come.
6059. vaiciyarkaḷil pūvaiciyar cirēṣṭam.
Of the Vaisyas the agriculturists are the chief.
6060. vaicūri vantavarkaḷ ammā eṉṟu kūppiṭa vēṇṭum.
Those who are attacked with small-pox must call it the goddess.
6061. vaitārai vāḻavaikkum vāḻntārait tāḻavaikkum.
It will confer on calumniaters prosperity, and reduce to affluent to
poverty.
6062. vaitīkar eṉṟāl pārppārukku peyar.
The term vaidikar is another name for brahmans.
6063. vaitīkam laukikam iraṇṭum vēṇṭum.
The sacred and the secular are both indispensable.
6064. vaitīkam eṉṟāl teyva camayam.
Vaidikam means the divine religion.
6065. vaitīkam āyntu aṟi.
Arrive at a knowledge of religion by studious investigation.
6066. vaittatu uṇṭāṉāl keṭṭatum uṇṭām.
If its being put there be true, its being lost may also be true.
6067. vaittatu kaṇṭatu collātē.
Tell not what has been kept or what you saw.
6068. vaittāl kuṭumi ciraittāl moṭṭai.
If the hair is left to grow, kudumi, if shaved, bald.
6069. vaittiyaṉ peritō vātti peritō?
Which is greater, a physician or a schoolmaster?
6070. vaittiyaṉukkum vāttikkum pētam illai.
A physician and a-schoolmaster never disagree.
6071. vaittiyaṉ ellārukkum potu.
A physician is common to all.
6072. vaittiyam vēṇṭātār ulakil illai.
There is no one on earth who does not require the services of a
physician.
6073. vaittiyam vāyāṭikkup palikkum.
A loquacious doctor is successful.
6074. vaittiyam ellām nampikkaiyāṟ palikkum.
Faith in medicine makes it effectual.
6075. vaittiyaṉukkum añcavēṇṭum, vampaṉukkum añcavēṇṭum.
One must fear a doctor as well as a traitor.
6076. vaittiyamō paittiyamō?
Is it medical skill or madness?
6077. vaittiyaṉ coṉṉatu ellām maruntu.
Whatever a physician prescribes is a remedy.
6078. vaittiyaṉukkut taṉ aviḻtam palikkātām.
It is said that a physician cannot cure himself.
6079. vaittiyaṉ takappaṉ pōla.
A physician is like a father.
6080. vaittiyaṉē peritu eṉpār cilar, vāttiyē peritu eṉpār cilar.
Some will say that a physician is greater than a schoolmaster, and
others, that a teacher is greater than a physician.
6081. vaittiyaṉ pārāta nōy tīrumā?
Can a disease be cured without treatment?
6082. vaittiyaṉ piḷḷai nōyiṉāl alla, maruntiṉāl cākum.
A doctor's child dies, not by disease, but by medicine.
6083. vaittiyaṉukku ūrār yāvarum ciṉēkitar.
The whole town is friendly to a physician.
6084. vaittiyattil iraṇa vaittiyamum, vayatil evvaṉamumnallatu.
As regards medical science, surgery - in regard to age, youth are
preferable.
6085. vaittiyaṉē uṉṉaiyē kuṇamākku.
Physician, heal thyself.
6086. vaittiyam ceytavaṉ ellām vaittiyaṉ.
Every medical practitioner is a physician.
6087. vaittiyam koñcamākilum teriyāta pērkaḷ illai.
There is none that does not know, at least, a little of medicine.
6088. vaittiyaṉ maruntilum kaimaruntē nalam.
Domestic medicine is preferable to that of a physician.
6089. vaittiyaṉ peritō maruntu peritō?
Which is greater, a physician or his medicine?
6090. vaittiyaṉ pēccu nālil oru paṅku.
But a fourth part of a quack's pretensions proves to be true.
6091. vaittiyaṉukku vantatu avaṉ talaiyōṭē.
The malady of a physician cleaves to him till death.
6092. vaittiya cāstiram cāstiraṅkaḷil vicēṣam.
Medical science is the most important of all sciences.
6093. vaittīcuraṉ kōvilukkup pōyum vayiṟṟu vali tīra illai.
His belly-ache is not cured even after going on a pilgrimage to
Vaidisvaran's temple.
6094. vaiyakattu uṟṟavaṉ meyyakam uṟṟavaṉ.
In all the world, he who is sincere is a friend.
6095. vaiyakattukkut tuṇai varataṉ kaḻal iṇai.
At the ankled feet of the giver of all good is found the refuge of the
world.
6096. vaiyakam oḻiyum vāṉ oḻiyum vallavar vacaṉam oḻiyātu.
Earth and heaven, will perish, but the words of the mighty will endure.
6097. vaiyakattil ellōrum oru pōkku alla.
All the world do not follow the same course.
6098. vaiyakattil poy collātavaṉ illai.
There is no one in the world, who has not uttered a falsehood.
6099. vaiyattuḷ nīti ceyyattakkatu.
It is proper to do justice in the world.
6100. vaiyattil uyarntōr cilar tāḻntōr palar.
In the world some are high, and many are low.
6101. vaiyattil uyarntōrkku irai tāḻntōr.
In the world the low are the victims of the high.
6102. vaiyattil uppukku ēmāṟiṉa pēr uṭampukkum ēmāṟuvārkaḷ.
On earth those who are disappointed of salt will meet with
disappointment as regards their body also.
6103. vaiyattil uppillāta pērkku uṭampu illai.
Op earth. those who have no salt have no body.
6104. vaiyattil uṭampu illāta pērkku uppu vēṇṭām.
Those on earth, who have not a body, have no occasion for salt.
6105. vaiyattil uppum vēṇṭum uṭalum vēṇṭum.
While on earth salt, and a body, are indispensable.
6106. vaiyattil uṭampu illāviṭṭālum uṭai vēṇṭum, paṇam illāviṭṭālum kaṉam
vēṇṭum.
Though destitute of personal beauty, clothing is needful, though
destitute of money reputation is necessary.
6107. vaiyattil vāḻvāṅku vāḻpavaṉ teyvattuḷ vaikkappaṭum.
He who lives as he ought in this world, will be ranked with the gods.
6108. vaiyattil mēlāṉa pērkkut tāḻmaiyāṉa maṉatu irukka vēṇṭum.
The great in the world must be distinguished by a humble mind.
6109. vaiyattil nallōr oruvaraik kaṇṭatu illai.
In all the world none really good, has been seen.
6110. vaiyattil nellukku iṟaitta nīr pullukkum pāyum.
Water drawn for the rice crop, benefits the grass also.
6111. vaiyattil nel arici vēṇṭātārum uṇṭu, pul arici cikkātārum uṇṭu.
In the world there are those who do not care for rice, as well as those
who can scarcely procure even the reeds of grass.
6112. vaiyattil caivaṉukkuc caivaṉmēl.
As a rule one saiva regards himself superior to another.
6113. vaiyattil vaiṣṇavaṉukku vaiṣṇavaṉ mēl.
One vaishnava thinks himself superior to another vaishnava.
6114. vaiyattil tantaiyilum tāy vicēṣam.
On earth a mother is more serviceable than a father.
6115. vaiyattil teviṭṭāta poruḷ aṉṉamum taṇṇīrum.
While on earth the things which do not cloy, are rice and water.
6116. vaiyattil nal viṉaiyāl ākātatu tīviṉaiyāl ākumā?
May that which cannot be accomplished by good deeds, be accomplished by
evil deeds ?
6117. vaiyattu maṉitar nālu vakai.
There are four kinds of men in the world.
6118. vaiyam tōṟum teyvam toḻu.
Worship God through all the world.
6119. vaiyam ēṟṟiṉ aiyam illai.
When the whole world applauds one, his merit is unquestionable.
6120. vaiyam ottāl aiyam illai.
If the world agree, there is no question about the matter.
6121. vaiyam pukaḻntāl aiyam illai.
When the whole world praise one, his character is unimpeachable.
6122. vaiyam peritāṉālum vaḷam uḷḷa iṭam koñcam.
Though the earth is of vast extent, the space adapted to the wants of
man is limited.
6123. vaiyam peritu atil varuttamum peritu.
The world is great, and itsanxieties are also great.
6124. vaiyam keṭṭāl aiyam illai.
If the world be destroyed, almsgiving will cease.
6125. vaiya vaiya vairakkal, tiṭṭat tiṭṭat tiṇṭukkal.
The more abused the more durable, the more despised the more hardened.
6126. vairattai vairam koṇṭē aṟukka vēṇṭum.
A diamond must be cut with a diamond.
6127. vairam maṉatil vaiyātē.
Harbour not malice.
6128. vairam koṇṭavaṉ vairappoṭi tiṉṟu cākiṟāṉ.
He who purchases diamonds will die by swallowing the particles.
6129. vairākkiya catakam catakaṅkaḷil vicēṣam.
The most distinguished of satakams is that on self control.
A satakan is a poem of a hundred stanzas.
6130. vairākkiyam pakai mutaliya turkkuṇaṅkaḷil vicēṣam.
Malice is of all forms of hatred the most pernicious.
6131. vaivārkku iṉpam illai, poṟuttārkkut tuṉpam illai.
The abusive have no happiness the forbearing, have no misery.
6132. vairāvi tuṟavikaḷil vicēṣam.
Among ascetics the vairavi is the most distinguished.
6133. veḷavālukku oru kaṉikku nūṟu kaṉi naṣṭam.
For every fruit consumed by a bat, a hundred are spoiled.
6134. veḷavālukku maramē kati, ataṉ kuñcukku atuvē kati.
Trees are the asylum of bats; bats are the refuge of their young.
6135. veḷavālukku etu tūram?
What place is too remote for a bat ?
6136. veḷavālukku iravil kaṇ teriyum.
Bats can see in the dark.
6137. veḷavālukku nīḷavum teriyum kuṟukavum teriyum.
Bats know how to extend or contract the body.
6138. veḷavālait tiṉṟālum aṇilait tiṉṉal ākātu.
Though one may feed on bats, he may not feed on squirrels.
6139. veḷavālaik koṉṟālum piṭiyai viṭātu.
A bat will not let go its hold though killed.
6140. veḷavālaip paṭci eṉṉalāmā?
May a bat be called a bird?
6141. veḷavālōṭu aṇiṟpiḷḷai cērumā?
Do squirrels mix themselves with bats?
6142. veḷavālō ciṟitu ataṉ aṭiyō valitu.
Small as the bat is, its stroke is powerful.
6143. veḷavāl aṭittut tiṉṉum, aṇil kaṭittut tiṉṉum.
Bats devour by striking, squirrels by nibbling.
6144. veḷavāl aṭikkup payappaṭalāmā?
May one fear the stroke of a bat?
6145. veḷavāl kūṭṭam kūṭṭamāka irukkum.
Bats are found in companies.
6146. veḷavāl talai narit talaipōl.
The head of a bat resembles that of a jackal.
6147. veḷavāl tiṉṉāta paḻam illai.
There is no fruit that a bat does not eat.
6148. veḷavāl pōlat toṅkukiṟāṉ.
He hangs like a bat.
6149. veḷavāl aṭitta paḻamum aṇil kaṭitta paḻamum taḷḷupaṭi ākumā?
Is fruit knocked off by bats, or nibbled by squirrels, rejected ?
6150. veḷavāl aṭaikiṟa vīṭṭil kuṭi iruppatu eppaṭi ?
How can one dwell in a house frequented by bats ?
Some suppose that bats in a house foretoken the speedy removal of
the resident family.
6151. veḷavi aṇil tottukiṟatu pōla.
As a squirrel ascends by clinging.
6152. veḷvic cērtta pōkkup poruḷ uṇṭu.
They have wealth who amassed it by plunder.
6153. veḷavil kuṭi irukkalāmā?
May one continue to dwell among those whom he robs?
6154. veḷavi veḷavic cērttālum maṟṟavarkku vaittu oḻivāṉ.
Though he may have amassed wealth by continued rapine, he will leave it
for others when he dies.
6155. veḷaviṉa pērkku muṭivatu curukku.
The rapacious end their days in the halter.
6156. veḷaviya karumam eṇṇit tuṇi.
Carefully persevere in what you undertake.
---------------
End of the collection of proverbs
